<script>
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// NEXUSCRM V3 — COMPLETE COMBINED SCRIPT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CORE CONFIG & STATE
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// A deployed backend (see backend/DEPLOY.md) lives on its own Workers
// URL, not this file's origin — so the backend address is a setting,
// not something derivable from window.location the way it was in
// local-only mode. Falls back to the old localhost:3000 dev convenience
// and same-origin guess when nothing has been configured yet.
function getConfiguredAPI() {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('nx_backend_url');
  if (saved) return saved.replace(/\/$/, '');
  return window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1'
    ? `${window.location.protocol}//${window.location.hostname}:3000/api`
    : `${window.location.origin}/api`;
}
let API = getConfiguredAPI();
let WS_URL = API.replace('/api', '').replace('https:', 'wss:').replace('http:', 'ws:');
// A backend URL explicitly configured in Settings = the user is committed to
// the real server. In that mode we NEVER silently fall back to localStorage —
// data would diverge and the user would think they're synced when they're not.
const REAL_MODE = () => !!localStorage.getItem('nx_backend_url');
function setBackendURL(url) {
  const clean = (url || '').trim().replace(/\/$/, '');
  if (clean) localStorage.setItem('nx_backend_url', clean);
  else localStorage.removeItem('nx_backend_url');
  API = getConfiguredAPI();
  WS_URL = API.replace('/api', '').replace('https:', 'wss:').replace('http:', 'ws:');
  BACKEND.available = null; // force a fresh health check against the new URL
}

const STATE = {
  token: localStorage.getItem('nx_token'),
  user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nx_user') || 'null'),
  view: 'dashboard',
  panelOpen: false,
  panelMessages: [],
  panelConvId: null,
  aiSettings: null,
  gmailConnected: false,
  gmailToken: localStorage.getItem('nx_gmail_token') || null,
  gmailPanelOpen: false,
  gmailEmails: [],
  aiVerified: null,
};

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// NEXUSCRM — LOCAL-FIRST ENGINE
// Runs entirely in this browser via localStorage. If a real
// server.js (see the Master Guide) is later started on
// localhost:3000, the app auto-detects it and switches to using
// it transparently — nothing else in this file needs to change.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── BACKEND DETECTION ──────────────────────────────────────────
const BACKEND = { available: null, checked: 0 };
let pingTimer = null;
async function pingBackend() {
  // Cloudflare Workers cold starts routinely take 1-5s — a 1.2s timeout used
  // to make the very first health check fail and derail the whole session
  // into local mode. 5s is the real-world-safe window.
  try {
    const ctrl = new AbortController();
    const t = setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(), 5000);
    const r = await fetch(API + '/health', { signal: ctrl.signal });
    clearTimeout(t);
    let body = null;
    try { body = await r.json(); } catch { /* non-JSON response — treated as not a real backend below */ }
    // A real backend answers /health with real JSON and no localOnly flag.
    // The bundled local static server (server.js, launched by
    // Start-NexusCRM.bat) intentionally answers /health with `localOnly:true`
    // to say "I'm not a real API — don't route real calls to me." Trusting
    // only `r.ok` here ignored that flag entirely: the local server would
    // report healthy, every subsequent api() call would get routed to it,
    // and since it has no real routes it silently served the whole HTML
    // file back with a 200 status for any path — realFetch() would then
    // parse that as an empty `{}` "success" for login, contacts, AI, every
    // feature at once, with no visible error. This check is what actually
    // prevents that: a healthy-but-local-only server must NOT be treated
    // as a real backend.
    BACKEND.available = !!(r.ok && body && body.ok && !body.localOnly);
  } catch { BACKEND.available = false; }
  BACKEND.checked = Date.now();
  // If a backend is configured but down, quietly retry every 15s so the app
  // recovers on its own once the worker wakes up.
  if (REAL_MODE() && !BACKEND.available && !pingTimer) {
    pingTimer = setTimeout(() => { pingTimer = null; pingBackend(); }, 15000);
  }
  return BACKEND.available;
}

// ── LOCAL DATABASE (persisted to localStorage) ─────────────────
const DB_KEY = 'nx_local_db_v1';
let DB = null;
function loadDB() {
  if (DB) return DB;
  try { DB = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DB_KEY) || 'null'); } catch { DB = null; }
  if (!DB) DB = { users: [], workspaces: {}, sessions: {} };
  if (!DB.users) DB.users = [];
  if (!DB.workspaces) DB.workspaces = {};
  if (!DB.sessions) DB.sessions = {};
  return DB;
}
let saveTimer = null;
function saveDB() {
  clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(DB_KEY, JSON.stringify(DB)); } catch(e) { console.warn('local save failed', e); }
  }, 120);
}

// ── CRYPTO HELPERS (local-only accounts — not real server security) ──
async function sha256(str) {
  const subtle = (globalThis.crypto && crypto.subtle);
  if (!subtle) throw new Error('This browser context blocks cryptography (crypto.subtle is unavailable). Open the app via https:// or localhost to use local accounts.');
  const buf = await subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(str));
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
function randToken(n=24) {
  const a = new Uint8Array(n); crypto.getRandomValues(a);
  return [...a].map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}

// ── WORKSPACE (per-user data store) ─────────────────────────────
function blankWorkspace() {
  return {
    contacts: [], deals: [], tasks: [], messages: [], appointments: [],
    reviews: [], workflows: [], invoices: [], social: [], subAccounts: [],
    aiSettings: { provider:'nvidia', model:'nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct', temperature:0.7, max_tokens:2048, system_prompt:'', openai_key:'', nvidia_key:'', custom_base_url:'http://localhost:11434/v1', custom_key:'', proxy_url:'', custom_key_set:false, brand_voice:'' },
    smtp: { host:'', port:587, user:'', pass:'', from:'' },
    aiUsage: { total:0, today:0, todayDate:'', conversations:0, messages:0, tokens_today:0 },
    forms: [], formSubmissions: [], courses: [], funnels: [], affiliates: [], community: [],
    aiMemory: [],
    seq: {}
  };
}
function nextId(ws, kind) { ws.seq[kind] = (ws.seq[kind]||0)+1; return ws.seq[kind]; }
function getWorkspace(userId) {
  const db = loadDB();
  if (!db.workspaces[userId]) db.workspaces[userId] = blankWorkspace();
  return db.workspaces[userId];
}
function currentWorkspace() {
  const db = loadDB();
  const uid = db.sessions[STATE.token];
  if (!uid) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
  return getWorkspace(uid);
}

// ── AUTH ROUTES ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function localAuthRegister(body) {
  const db = loadDB();
  const email = (body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!email || !body.name || !body.password) throw new Error('All fields required');
  if (body.password.length < 8) throw new Error('Password must be at least 8 characters');
  if (db.users.find(u=>u.email===email)) throw new Error('An account with this email already exists');
  const salt = randToken(8);
  const passHash = await sha256(salt + body.password);
  const user = { id: 'u' + randToken(6), name: body.name, email, salt, passHash, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
  db.users.push(user);
  db.workspaces[user.id] = blankWorkspace();
  const token = randToken();
  db.sessions[token] = user.id;
  saveDB();
  return { token, user: { id:user.id, name:user.name, email:user.email } };
}
async function localAuthLogin(body) {
  const db = loadDB();
  const email = (body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const user = db.users.find(u=>u.email===email);
  if (!user) throw new Error('No account found with that email');
  const hash = await sha256(user.salt + body.password);
  if (hash !== user.passHash) throw new Error('Incorrect password');
  const token = randToken();
  db.sessions[token] = user.id;
  saveDB();
  return { token, user: { id:user.id, name:user.name, email:user.email } };
}
async function localAuthDemo() {
  // "Quick Start" — creates a real, empty, private local account with no
  // email/password required. It is NOT pre-loaded with sample data; every
  // number you see afterward reflects only what you actually enter.
  const db = loadDB();
  const id = 'guest_' + randToken(6);
  const user = { id, name: 'Guest', email: '', salt:'', passHash:'', createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
  db.users.push(user);
  db.workspaces[id] = blankWorkspace();
  const token = randToken();
  db.sessions[token] = id;
  saveDB();
  return { token, user: { id:user.id, name:user.name, email:user.email } };
}
function localAuthMe() {
  const db = loadDB();
  const uid = db.sessions[STATE.token];
  if (!uid) throw new Error('Session expired');
  const user = db.users.find(u=>u.id===uid);
  if (!user) throw new Error('Session expired');
  return { id:user.id, name:user.name, email:user.email };
}

// ── ENRICHMENT HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────
function contactName(ws, id) { const c = ws.contacts.find(x=>x.id===id); return c ? c.name : null; }
function withContactName(ws, obj) { return { ...obj, contact_name: obj.contact_id ? contactName(ws, obj.contact_id) : (obj.contact_name||null) }; }

// ── STATS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function computeStats(ws) {
  const openDeals = ws.deals.filter(d=>!['won','lost'].includes(d.stage));
  const wonDeals = ws.deals.filter(d=>d.stage==='won');
  const now = new Date();
  const upcomingAppts = ws.appointments.filter(a=>a.status==='scheduled' && new Date(a.date) >= new Date(now.toDateString()));
  const pendingTasks = ws.tasks.filter(t=>t.status==='todo');
  const paidInvoices = ws.invoices.filter(i=>i.status==='paid');
  return {
    contacts: ws.contacts.length,
    pipeline_value: openDeals.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.value,0),
    open_deals: openDeals.length,
    won_revenue: wonDeals.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.value,0),
    upcoming_appointments: upcomingAppts.length,
    pending_tasks: pendingTasks.length,
    revenue_collected: paidInvoices.reduce((a,i)=>a+i.total,0),
    sub_accounts: ws.subAccounts.length
  };
}

// ── LEAD SCORING (deterministic, explainable local engine) ─────
function scoreLead(ws, c) {
  let score = 10, reasons = [];
  const stageW = { customer:35, won:35, negotiation:28, proposal:22, qualified:16, prospect:8, lead:2, lost:-20 };
  score += stageW[c.stage] || 0;
  if ((stageW[c.stage]||0) >= 16) reasons.push(`${c.stage} stage`);
  if (c.email) score += 8;
  if (c.phone) { score += 8; reasons.push('has phone + email on file'); }
  const deals = ws.deals.filter(d=>d.contact_id===c.id);
  if (deals.length) {
    const val = deals.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.value,0);
    score += Math.min(25, Math.round(val/1000));
    reasons.push(`${deals.length} deal(s) worth $${val.toLocaleString()}`);
  }
  const daysSince = c.updated_at ? Math.floor((Date.now()-new Date(c.updated_at).getTime())/86400000) : 999;
  if (daysSince <= 3) { score += 10; reasons.push('active in the last 3 days'); }
  else if (daysSince > 30) { score -= 10; reasons.push('no activity in 30+ days'); }
  if (c.notes && c.notes.length > 20) score += 4;
  score = Math.max(1, Math.min(99, Math.round(score)));
  const reason = reasons.length ? `Scored ${score}/100 — ${reasons.join(', ')}.` : `Scored ${score}/100 based on available signals.`;
  return { score, reason };
}

// ── AI USAGE TRACKING ────────────────────────────────────────────
function trackAIUsage(ws, kind) {
  const today = new Date().toDateString();
  if (ws.aiUsage.todayDate !== today) { ws.aiUsage.today = 0; ws.aiUsage.todayDate = today; }
  ws.aiUsage.total++; ws.aiUsage.today++;
  if (kind === 'chat') ws.aiUsage.conversations++;
  ws.aiUsage.messages++;
  saveDB();
}

// ── LOCAL TEMPLATE FALLBACKS (used when no AI key / provider unreachable) ──
const LOCAL_TPL = {
  email: (ctx,tone,target) => `Subject: Following up\n\nHi${target?' '+target:''},\n\nHope you're doing well. Wanted to follow up regarding ${ctx||'our recent conversation'}. Let me know if you have a few minutes this week to connect — happy to work around your schedule.\n\nLooking forward to hearing from you.\n\nBest regards`,
  whatsapp: (ctx,tone,target) => `Hi${target?' '+target:''}! Just following up on ${ctx||'our conversation'} 🙂 Let me know if you have any questions — happy to help whenever works for you!`,
  sms: (ctx) => `Hi! Quick follow-up on ${ctx||'your request'}. Reply anytime — happy to help.`,
  social_linkedin: (ctx) => `Excited to share an update on ${ctx||'our work'}. We've been focused on delivering real results for our clients, and it's paying off. Here's what we've learned along the way...\n\n#business #growth`,
  social_twitter: (ctx) => `Quick thought on ${ctx||'today'}: the details matter more than most people think. Small consistent improvements compound. 🚀`,
  social_instagram: (ctx) => `✨ ${ctx||'Behind the scenes'} ✨\nGrateful for this journey and the people who make it possible. More to come!\n.\n.\n#smallbusiness #growth`,
  social_facebook: (ctx,tone) => `📣 ${ctx||'Big news from our team'}!\n\nWe wanted to share something we've been working on — ${ctx||'an update worth your attention'}. What do you think? Drop a comment and let's talk! 👇\n\n#business #growth`,
  cold_email: (ctx,tone,target) => `Subject: Quick idea for ${target||'your team'}\n\nHi${target?' '+target:''},\n\nI noticed ${ctx||'you might be facing a challenge we solve'}. I'd love to share a 10-minute walkthrough — no pressure at all.\n\nWorth a quick chat this week?\n\nBest regards`,
  followup_email: (ctx,tone,target) => `Subject: Following up\n\nHi${target?' '+target:''},\n\nCircling back on ${ctx||'our last conversation'} — wanted to keep the ball rolling. Happy to jump on a call whenever suits you.\n\nThanks!`,
  landing_page: (ctx) => `# ${ctx||'Your Product'}\n\nThe simplest way to ${ctx||'solve your problem'} — no clutter, no learning curve.\n\n## What you get\n- Instant setup\n- Proven results\n- Support that answers\n\n[ Get Started ]`,
  hashtags: (ctx) => `#${(ctx||'business').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'')} #growth #smallbusiness #marketing #tips #success #entrepreneur #startup #productivity #automation #digital #strategy #results #community #learn`,
  blog: (ctx) => `# ${ctx||'A Look at What We Do'}\n\nIn today's fast-moving business landscape, staying ahead means being intentional about ${ctx||'your approach'}. In this post, we break down the key ideas, share a few lessons learned, and offer practical next steps you can apply right away.\n\n## Key Takeaways\n- Focus on what actually moves the needle\n- Measure consistently\n- Iterate quickly\n\nThanks for reading — more insights coming soon.`,
  ad_copy: (ctx) => `Headline: Get Results, Not Excuses\nBody: ${ctx||'Our solution'} helps you save time and grow faster. Try it today — no risk, real results.\nCTA: Get Started Free`,
  proposal: (ctx) => `PROPOSAL\n\nOverview:\n${ctx||'Project scope to be defined based on our conversation.'}\n\nDeliverables:\n- Discovery & planning\n- Execution\n- Review & handoff\n\nTimeline: 2-4 weeks\nInvestment: To be confirmed based on final scope\n\nWe're excited about the opportunity to work together.`,
  review_reply: (ctx) => ctx && /late|slow|mess|wrong|bad|disappoint|refund/i.test(ctx)
    ? `Thank you for the honest feedback — I'm sorry this didn't meet expectations. I'd like to make it right; please reach out directly so we can resolve this quickly.`
    : `Thank you so much for the kind words! We really appreciate you taking the time to share this and look forward to working with you again.`,
  sms_generic: (ctx) => `Hi! Quick note about ${ctx||'your account'} — reach out anytime with questions.`
};
function localGenerate(type, context, tone, target) {
  const fn = LOCAL_TPL[type] || LOCAL_TPL.email;
  return fn(context, tone, target);
}
function localComplete(prompt) {
  const p = (prompt||'').toLowerCase();
  if (p.includes('pipeline') && p.includes('recommend')) return `1. Prioritize the highest-value deals sitting in "proposal" or "negotiation" — they're closest to closing.\n2. Any deal untouched for 14+ days should get a direct check-in call today.\n3. For deals under $2,000, consider bundling or upselling rather than spending equal time per deal.`;
  if (p.includes('tasks') && p.includes('focus')) return `Focus on urgent and high-priority tasks with the nearest due dates first. Batch similar tasks (calls, follow-ups) together to reduce context-switching.`;
  if (p.includes('sentiment')) return `Overall tone reads as neutral-to-positive with no strong negative signals.`;
  if (p.includes('objection')) return `1) Empathize: acknowledge the concern directly.\n2) Reframe: connect price to the value/outcome they get.\n3) Social proof: reference a similar customer's results.\n\nScript: "I hear you — let's talk about what this actually saves you over the next 6 months..."`;
  if (p.includes('summar')) return `Key points, decisions, and open questions have been noted. Next steps: confirm ownership and timelines with the relevant stakeholders.`;
  if (p.includes('translate')) return `(Local mode has no live translation engine — connect an AI provider in Settings for real translation.)`;
  if (p.includes('research') || p.includes('market')) return `Market Overview: Growing steadily with increasing demand for automation.\nKey Players: A handful of established platforms plus emerging niche tools.\nOpportunity: Underserved small-business segment wanting simple, affordable tools.\nRecommended strategy: Differentiate on ease of use and price transparency.`;
  if (p.includes('executive') || p.includes('report')) return `Business Health: Stable with healthy pipeline coverage.\nTop wins: Deals closing on schedule, task completion trending up.\nConcerns: A few stalled deals need direct attention.\nRecommendations: Focus outreach on qualified/proposal-stage deals this month.`;
  if (p.includes('community')) return `1. "What's the #1 tool in your stack right now?" — sparks comparison threads\n2. Share a quick customer win story — builds social proof\n3. "Poll: biggest bottleneck in your workflow?" — drives replies\n4. Behind-the-scenes look at a recent build — humanizes the brand\n5. "Ask me anything" thread — deepens engagement`;
  if (p.includes('saas') && p.includes('setup')) return `Pricing Tiers: Starter $49/mo, Pro $149/mo, Agency $399/mo.\nOnboarding: guided setup call + welcome email sequence + template library.\nSupport: shared help center + priority chat for Pro/Agency.\nScaling: automate billing via Stripe, self-serve signup after first 10 manual clients.\nFirst 10 clients: outreach to your existing network, local business Facebook groups, and a limited-time founding-member discount.`;
  if (p.includes('pricing strategy')) return `Tier 1 — Starter ($49/mo): core CRM, 500 contacts.\nTier 2 — Pro ($149/mo): AI features, unlimited contacts, automations.\nTier 3 — Agency ($399/mo): white-label, sub-accounts, priority support.\nUpsell path: start clients on Starter, show AI feature value, migrate to Pro within 60 days.`;
  if (p.includes('funnel') && p.includes('optimiz')) return `1. Tighten the headline to lead with the outcome, not the feature.\n2. Cut form fields to the essential 2-3 — every extra field drops conversion.\n3. Add one clear testimonial above the fold.\n4. Retarget drop-offs with a follow-up email within 1 hour.`;
  if (p.includes('form')) return `Recommended fields: Name, Email, Phone (optional), one qualifying question ("What are you looking for help with?"). Keep it to 4 fields max — shorter forms convert significantly better.`;
  if (p.includes('affiliate')) return `Subject: Partner with us — earn 20% recurring commission\n\nHi there,\n\nWe're inviting a small group of partners to promote our platform in exchange for a 20% recurring commission on every referral. You'll get a dedicated tracking link, marketing assets, and monthly payouts.\n\nInterested? Reply and I'll get you set up in minutes.`;
  return `Here's a first pass based on what you shared. Connect a live AI provider in Settings → AI Providers for deeper, more specific output.`;
}
function localSentiment(text) {
  const pos = ['great','love','excellent','happy','amazing','good','thank','fantastic','awesome','perfect'];
  const neg = ['bad','late','slow','angry','terrible','worst','disappoint','refund','broke','issue','problem','complain'];
  const t = (text||'').toLowerCase();
  let score = 0;
  pos.forEach(w=>{ if(t.includes(w)) score++; });
  neg.forEach(w=>{ if(t.includes(w)) score--; });
  const sentiment = score>0?'positive':score<0?'negative':'neutral';
  const confidence = Math.min(95, 60 + Math.abs(score)*10);
  return { sentiment, confidence };
}
function localBuildWorkflow(goal) {
  const g = (goal||'').toLowerCase();
  let trigger = 'manual';
  if (/new (lead|contact)/.test(g)) trigger = 'new_contact';
  else if (/deal|stage|won|pipeline/.test(g)) trigger = 'deal_stage_change';
  else if (/appointment|book/.test(g)) trigger = 'appointment_booked';
  else if (/invoice|paid|payment/.test(g)) trigger = 'invoice_paid';
  else if (/form/.test(g)) trigger = 'form_submitted';
  const steps = [];
  if (/whatsapp/.test(g)) steps.push({ action:'send_whatsapp', note:'Send WhatsApp message' });
  if (/email/.test(g)) steps.push({ action:'send_email', note:'Send email' });
  if (/task|follow.?up/.test(g)) steps.push({ action:'create_task', note:'Create follow-up task' });
  if (/stage|move|prospect|qualif/.test(g)) steps.push({ action:'update_stage', note:'Move to next stage' });
  if (!steps.length) steps.push({ action:'create_task', note:'Review and follow up' });
  const name = goal.length > 48 ? goal.slice(0,45)+'...' : (goal || 'New Workflow');
  return { name, trigger, steps };
}

// ── REAL AI PROVIDER CALLS (direct from browser, OpenAI-compatible) ──
//
// IMPORTANT BACKGROUND — why "NVIDIA NIM: failed to fetch" happens:
// integrate.api.nvidia.com (and api.openai.com) do NOT send the CORS
// headers a browser requires to let a webpage call them directly. This is
// on the provider's side, confirmed by NVIDIA's own developer forum
// (a user hit the exact same "failed to fetch" calling this endpoint from
// fetch(), and NVIDIA staff acknowledged there's currently no CORS support
// for direct browser calls). No amount of fixing THIS app's code can make
// a browser bypass that — CORS is enforced by the browser, not the app.
// The fix is either (a) route the request through a tiny proxy server that
// adds the missing CORS headers (see buildProviderRequest's proxy_url
// support + the "Fix Connection" guide in Settings), or (b) run a local
// OpenAI-compatible server (Ollama, LM Studio) that allows browser origins.
//
// Builds the {url, key, model} triple for whichever provider is active,
// transparently routing through an optional CORS proxy if one is configured.
function buildProviderRequest(s) {
  let direct, key, model;
  if (s.provider === 'openai') {
    if (!s.openai_key) return null;
    direct = 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions'; key = s.openai_key; model = s.model || 'gpt-4o-mini';
  } else if (s.provider === 'nvidia') {
    if (!s.nvidia_key) return null;
    direct = 'https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions'; key = s.nvidia_key; model = s.model || 'nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct';
  } else if (s.provider === 'custom') {
    direct = (s.custom_base_url||'http://localhost:11434/v1').replace(/\/$/,'') + '/chat/completions';
    key = s.custom_key || ''; model = s.model || 'llama3.1';
  } else return null;
  const url = s.proxy_url ? s.proxy_url.replace(/\/$/,'') + '?url=' + encodeURIComponent(direct) : direct;
  return { url, key, model, direct, viaProxy: !!s.proxy_url };
}

// Turns a raw fetch()/network failure into an explanation that actually
// tells the user what to do, instead of the browser's generic message.
function friendlyFetchError(e, req) {
  if (e?.name === 'AbortError') return new Error('Timed out waiting for a response from the provider.');
  if (e instanceof TypeError) {
    return new Error(
      (req?.viaProxy
        ? "Couldn't reach your CORS proxy — check the Proxy URL in Settings and that the proxy is deployed and reachable."
        : "Couldn't reach the AI provider directly from the browser. This is almost always the provider blocking direct browser requests (CORS), not your API key or settings.")
      + ' Open Settings → AI Providers → "Fix Connection" for a 2-minute proxy setup, or switch to a local Custom provider with CORS enabled.'
    );
  }
  return e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e));
}

// Turns an HTTP error response into a specific, actionable message.
async function friendlyHttpError(r) {
  let body = null;
  try { body = await r.json(); } catch {}
  // NVIDIA (and others) sometimes use RFC-7807 style: { type, title, status, detail }
  const msg = body?.error?.message || body?.message || body?.detail;
  if (r.status === 401 || r.status === 403) return new Error(msg || 'Invalid or unauthorized API key — double check it in Settings.');
  if (r.status === 404) return new Error(msg || 'Model not found for this provider — check the model name.');
  if (r.status === 410) return new Error(msg || 'That model has been retired by the provider (end of life) — pick a different model in Settings.');
  if (r.status === 429) return new Error(msg || "Rate limited or out of free credits — wait a bit and try again.");
  if (r.status >= 500) return new Error(msg || `The provider's server had an error (${r.status}) — try again shortly.`);
  return new Error(msg || `Provider error ${r.status}`);
}

async function callProviderDirect(ws, messages, opts) {
  const s = ws.aiSettings;
  const req = buildProviderRequest(s);
  if (!req) throw new Error('no_key');
  if (s.system_prompt) messages = [{ role:'system', content:s.system_prompt }, ...messages.filter(m=>m.role!=='system')];
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(), 25000);
  let r;
  try {
    r = await fetch(req.url, {
      method:'POST', signal: ctrl.signal,
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', ...(req.key?{Authorization:`Bearer ${req.key}`}:{}) },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model: req.model, messages, temperature: opts?.temperature ?? s.temperature ?? 0.7, max_tokens: opts?.max_tokens ?? s.max_tokens ?? 1024 })
    });
  } catch(e) { clearTimeout(t); throw friendlyFetchError(e, req); }
  clearTimeout(t);
  if (!r.ok) throw await friendlyHttpError(r);
  const d = await r.json();
  return d.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '';
}

// True token-by-token streaming, instead of waiting for the whole reply
// and typing it out afterward. Returns a {body: ReadableStream} shaped the
// same way the local-fallback and real-backend paths already return, so
// no calling UI code needs to change.
async function streamProviderDirect(ws, messages, contextData) {
  const s = ws.aiSettings;
  const req = buildProviderRequest(s);
  if (!req) throw new Error('no_key');
  const sys = contextData ? `Context: ${contextData}` : (s.system_prompt || '');
  if (sys) messages = [{ role:'system', content: sys }, ...messages.filter(m=>m.role!=='system')];

  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(), 45000);
  let r;
  try {
    r = await fetch(req.url, {
      method:'POST', signal: ctrl.signal,
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', ...(req.key?{Authorization:`Bearer ${req.key}`}:{}) },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model: req.model, messages, temperature: s.temperature ?? 0.7, max_tokens: s.max_tokens ?? 1024, stream: true })
    });
  } catch(e) { clearTimeout(t); throw friendlyFetchError(e, req); }
  clearTimeout(t);
  if (!r.ok || !r.body) throw await friendlyHttpError(r);

  const reader = r.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  let buf = '';
  const stream = new ReadableStream({
    async pull(controller) {
      let value, done;
      try { ({ value, done } = await reader.read()); }
      catch(e) { controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify({done:true})}\n\n`)); controller.close(); return; }
      if (done) { controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify({done:true})}\n\n`)); controller.close(); return; }
      buf += decoder.decode(value, { stream:true });
      const lines = buf.split('\n');
      buf = lines.pop(); // keep any partial trailing line for the next chunk
      for (const raw of lines) {
        const line = raw.trim();
        if (!line.startsWith('data:')) continue;
        const payload = line.slice(5).trim();
        if (payload === '[DONE]') continue;
        try {
          const j = JSON.parse(payload);
          const delta = j.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
          if (delta) controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify({delta})}\n\n`));
        } catch {}
      }
    }
  });
  return { body: stream };
}

function hasAnyKey(ws) {
  const s = ws.aiSettings;
  return (s.provider==='openai' && !!s.openai_key) || (s.provider==='nvidia' && !!s.nvidia_key) || (s.provider==='custom');
}

// ── UNIFIED AI OPS (try real provider, fall back to local templates) ──
const LOCAL_FALLBACK_NOTE = '⚠️ Local draft (no AI provider connected) — connect a key in Settings → AI Providers for a real AI-written version.\n\n';
// A key being configured but the CALL failing (almost always CORS, see
// buildProviderRequest's comment) is a completely different situation
// from no key being set at all — telling the user "no AI provider
// connected" when they've correctly added a key is actively misleading
// and sends them chasing the wrong problem. This surfaces the real,
// specific reason (which already includes actionable next steps, see
// friendlyFetchError/friendlyHttpError) instead.
function localFallbackNote(errorMsg) {
  if (!errorMsg) return LOCAL_FALLBACK_NOTE;
  return `⚠️ Using a local draft — your AI provider is configured but couldn't be reached:\n${errorMsg}\n\n`;
}
async function aiOpComplete(ws, prompt, opts) {
  trackAIUsage(ws,'op');
  if (hasAnyKey(ws)) {
    try { const content = await callProviderDirect(ws, [{role:'user',content:prompt}], opts); return { content, live:true }; }
    catch(e) { return { content: localFallbackNote(e.message) + localComplete(prompt), live:false, error: e.message }; }
  }
  return { content: LOCAL_FALLBACK_NOTE + localComplete(prompt), live:false };
}
async function aiOpGenerate(ws, {type,context,tone,target}) {
  trackAIUsage(ws,'op');
  const prompt = `Write a ${type||'email'} message. ${tone?`Tone: ${tone}.`:''} ${target?`Audience: ${target}.`:''} Context: ${context||'general business follow-up'}. Return only the message content, no preamble.` + (ws.aiSettings?.brand_voice ? `\n\nWrite in this brand voice: ${ws.aiSettings.brand_voice}` : '');
  if (hasAnyKey(ws)) {
    try { const content = await callProviderDirect(ws, [{role:'user',content:prompt}], {}); return { content, live:true }; }
    catch(e) { return { content: localFallbackNote(e.message) + localGenerate(type, context, tone, target), live:false, error: e.message }; }
  }
  return { content: LOCAL_FALLBACK_NOTE + localGenerate(type, context, tone, target), live:false };
}
async function aiOpSentiment(ws, text) {
  trackAIUsage(ws,'op');
  if (hasAnyKey(ws)) {
    try {
      const content = await callProviderDirect(ws, [{role:'user',content:`Classify the sentiment of this message as exactly one word (positive, negative, or neutral), then a confidence percentage 0-100, separated by a comma. Message: "${text}"`}], {max_tokens:20});
      const m = content.match(/(positive|negative|neutral)\D*(\d{1,3})?/i);
      if (m) return { sentiment: m[1].toLowerCase(), confidence: Math.min(99, parseInt(m[2])||80) };
    } catch(e) {}
  }
  return localSentiment(text);
}
async function aiOpScoreLead(ws, contact) {
  trackAIUsage(ws,'op');
  const local = scoreLead(ws, contact);
  if (hasAnyKey(ws)) {
    try {
      const prompt = `Score this sales lead from 0-100 based on likelihood to close, then give a one-sentence reason. Lead: stage=${contact.stage}, has_email=${!!contact.email}, has_phone=${!!contact.phone}, notes="${contact.notes||''}". Respond as: SCORE, REASON`;
      const content = await callProviderDirect(ws, [{role:'user',content:prompt}], {max_tokens:120});
      const m = content.match(/(\d{1,3})[,\s-]+(.*)/s);
      if (m) return { score: Math.max(1,Math.min(99,parseInt(m[1]))), reason: m[2].trim(), live: true };
    } catch(e) { return { ...local, live: false, error: e.message }; }
  }
  return { ...local, live: false };
}
async function aiOpBuildWorkflow(ws, goal) {
  trackAIUsage(ws,'op');
  if (hasAnyKey(ws)) {
    try {
      const prompt = `Design a CRM automation workflow for this goal: "${goal}". Respond ONLY as JSON: {"name":"...","trigger":"new_contact|deal_stage_change|appointment_booked|invoice_paid|form_submitted|manual","steps":[{"action":"send_whatsapp|send_email|create_task|update_stage","note":"..."}]}`;
      const content = await callProviderDirect(ws, [{role:'user',content:prompt}], {max_tokens:400});
      const jsonMatch = content.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
      if (jsonMatch) { const w = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]); if (w.name && w.trigger) return { ...w, live: true }; }
    } catch(e) { return { ...localBuildWorkflow(goal), live: false, error: e.message }; }
  }
  return { ...localBuildWorkflow(goal), live: false };
}
function aiInsightsDashboard(ws) {
  const insights = [];
  const stalled = ws.deals.filter(d=>!['won','lost'].includes(d.stage));
  if (stalled.length) {
    const top = [...stalled].sort((a,b)=>b.value-a.value)[0];
    insights.push({ priority:'high', title:'Biggest open opportunity', insight:`"${top.title}" (${contactName(ws,top.contact_id)||'no contact'}) is worth $${top.value.toLocaleString()} at ${top.probability||0}% probability.`, action:'Review pipeline' });
  }
  const overdue = ws.tasks.filter(t=>t.status==='todo' && t.due_date && new Date(t.due_date) < new Date());
  if (overdue.length) insights.push({ priority:'high', title:`${overdue.length} overdue task${overdue.length>1?'s':''}`, insight:`${overdue.map(t=>t.title).slice(0,2).join(', ')}${overdue.length>2?', ...':''} ${overdue.length>1?'are':'is'} past due.`, action:'Go to Tasks' });
  const unscored = ws.contacts.filter(c=>!c.ai_score && ['qualified','proposal','negotiation'].includes(c.stage));
  if (unscored.length) insights.push({ priority:'medium', title:'Unscored active leads', insight:`${unscored.length} contact(s) in active stages have no AI score yet.`, action:'Go to Contacts' });
  const now = new Date();
  const soon = ws.appointments.filter(a=>a.status==='scheduled' && new Date(a.date) >= now && new Date(a.date) <= new Date(now.getTime()+7*86400000));
  if (soon.length) insights.push({ priority:'low', title:`${soon.length} appointment${soon.length>1?'s':''} this week`, insight:`Next up: ${soon[0].title} on ${soon[0].date}.`, action:'Go to Calendar' });
  if (!insights.length) insights.push({ priority:'low', title:'All clear', insight:'No urgent items right now — nice work staying on top of things.', action:'Go to Dashboard' });
  return insights.slice(0,3);
}
// Real connectivity test — actually calls each configured provider with a
// minimal request rather than just checking whether a key string exists.
async function pingProvider(provider, s) {
  // Build against a synthetic per-provider settings object so all three
  // providers can be tested in parallel regardless of which one is "active".
  const testS = { ...s, provider };
  if (provider === 'custom' && s.provider !== 'custom') return { status:'no_key' };
  // Per-provider model: never test NVIDIA with an OpenAI model (or vice
  // versa) — that produced false "model not found" errors with valid keys.
  const cur = String(s.model || '');
  const looksNvidia = /^[a-z0-9_-]+\/[a-z0-9._-]+$/i.test(cur);
  if (provider === 'nvidia') testS.model = looksNvidia ? cur : 'nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct';
  if (provider === 'openai') testS.model = looksNvidia ? 'gpt-4o-mini' : (cur || 'gpt-4o-mini');
  if (provider === 'custom') testS.model = cur || 'llama3.1';
  const req = buildProviderRequest(testS);
  if (!req) return { status:'no_key' };

  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(), 10000);
  try {
    const r = await fetch(req.url, {
      method:'POST', signal: ctrl.signal,
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', ...(req.key?{Authorization:`Bearer ${req.key}`}:{}) },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model: req.model, messages:[{role:'user',content:'Reply with the single word: ok'}], max_tokens:5 })
    });
    clearTimeout(t);
    if (r.ok) { const d = await r.json().catch(()=>({})); return { status:'ok', model: d?.model || req.model }; }
    return { status:'error', message: (await friendlyHttpError(r)).message, kind:'http' };
  } catch(e) {
    clearTimeout(t);
    // Classify the browser-blocked case (local-only mode + NVIDIA/OpenAI):
    // this is EXPECTED, not a key problem — the UI explains it separately.
    const corsLocal = (e instanceof TypeError) && !req.viaProxy && (provider === 'nvidia' || provider === 'openai') && !REAL_MODE() && !BACKEND.available;
    return { status:'error', message: friendlyFetchError(e, req).message, kind: corsLocal ? 'cors_local' : 'network' };
  }
}
async function aiHealth(ws) {
  const s = ws.aiSettings;
  const [openai, nvidia, custom] = await Promise.all([
    pingProvider('openai', s), pingProvider('nvidia', s), pingProvider('custom', s)
  ]);
  return { openai, nvidia, custom };
}

// ── LOCAL CHAT (offline fallback used by all 3 chat UIs when no provider
// is reachable — real-provider replies are handled upstream in
// chatStreamFetch via true token streaming, see streamProviderDirect) ──
async function localChatComplete(ws, messages, contextData) {
  const last = (messages[messages.length-1]?.content || '').toLowerCase();
  if (/hot|best|top.*lead/.test(last)) {
    const top = [...ws.contacts].filter(c=>c.ai_score).sort((a,b)=>b.ai_score-a.ai_score).slice(0,3);
    return top.length ? `Your hottest leads right now:\n\n${top.map(c=>`• ${c.name} (${c.ai_score}/100) — ${c.stage}`).join('\n')}` : `No scored leads yet — try "Score Lead" from a contact's detail view.`;
  }
  if (/pipeline|deals?/.test(last)) {
    const stats = computeStats(ws);
    return `Pipeline snapshot: $${stats.pipeline_value.toLocaleString()} across ${stats.open_deals} open deals, $${stats.won_revenue.toLocaleString()} won so far.`;
  }
  if (/task/.test(last)) {
    const pending = ws.tasks.filter(t=>t.status==='todo');
    return pending.length ? `You have ${pending.length} open task(s). Nearest due: "${pending[0].title}" (${pending[0].due_date||'no date'}).` : `All caught up — no open tasks!`;
  }
  if (/contact|customer/.test(last)) {
    return `You have ${ws.contacts.length} contacts on file. Want me to point you to your hottest leads or your pipeline instead?`;
  }
  return `I'm running in local mode (no AI key configured yet). Add a free NVIDIA NIM or OpenAI key in Settings → AI Providers for full conversational answers — meanwhile I can tell you about your leads, pipeline, or tasks if you ask directly.`;
}

// A drop-in replacement for `fetch(API + '/ai/chat/stream', {...})` that
// returns the same {body: {getReader()}} shape the UI code expects,
// whether backed by a real server or the local engine.
// Wraps plain text into the same {body: ReadableStream} SSE shape a real
// streaming provider returns, playing it back word-by-word so the UI still
// feels alive when there's no live provider to stream from.
function typewriterStream(fullText) {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const words = fullText.split(/(\s+)/);
  let i = 0;
  const stream = new ReadableStream({
    async pull(controller) {
      if (i >= words.length) {
        controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify({done:true})}\n\n`));
        controller.close();
        return;
      }
      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, 14));
      controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify({delta: words[i]})}\n\n`));
      i++;
    }
  });
  return { body: stream };
}

async function chatStreamFetch(messages, contextData) {
  if (REAL_MODE() || BACKEND.available) {
    return fetch(API + '/ai/chat/stream', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', Authorization:`Bearer ${STATE.token}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ messages, context_data: contextData })
    });
  }
  if (BACKEND.available === null) await pingBackend();
  if (BACKEND.available) {
    return fetch(API + '/ai/chat/stream', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', Authorization:`Bearer ${STATE.token}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ messages, context_data: contextData })
    });
  }
  const ws = currentWorkspace();
  trackAIUsage(ws, 'chat');

  if (hasAnyKey(ws)) {
    try {
      // Real, true token-by-token streaming straight from the configured
      // provider - first tokens appear as soon as the provider sends them.
      return await streamProviderDirect(ws, messages, contextData);
    } catch (e) {
      // Surface the REAL reason (e.g. a CORS block) instead of silently
      // reverting to canned local answers with no explanation.
      let local;
      try { local = await localChatComplete(ws, messages, contextData); }
      catch { local = "I can still help using local data - try asking about your leads, pipeline, or tasks."; }
      return typewriterStream(`⚠️ Couldn't reach ${ws.aiSettings.provider}: ${e.message}\n\n${local}`);
    }
  }

  let fullText;
  try { fullText = await localChatComplete(ws, messages, contextData); }
  catch(e) { fullText = "⚠️ Something went wrong generating a reply locally."; }
  return typewriterStream(fullText);
}

// ── ROUTER ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function localApi(path, method='GET', body=null) {
  await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, 50 + Math.random()*70)); // realistic latency feel
  const [rawPath, qs] = path.split('?');
  const q = new URLSearchParams(qs||'');
  const parts = rawPath.split('/').filter(Boolean);
  const root = parts[0];

  // ── AUTH (no session required) ──
  if (rawPath === '/health') return { ok:true };
  if (rawPath === '/auth/register' && method==='POST') return localAuthRegister(body||{});
  if (rawPath === '/auth/login' && method==='POST') return localAuthLogin(body||{});
  if (rawPath === '/auth/demo' && method==='POST') return localAuthDemo();
  if (rawPath === '/auth/me') return localAuthMe();

  // everything below requires a session
  const ws = currentWorkspace();

  // ── CONTACTS ──
  if (root === 'contacts') {
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') {
        let list = ws.contacts;
        const search = (q.get('search')||'').toLowerCase();
        const stage = q.get('stage');
        if (search) list = list.filter(c => (c.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (c.email||'').toLowerCase().includes(search) || (c.company||'').toLowerCase().includes(search));
        if (stage) list = list.filter(c => c.stage === stage);
        const tag = q.get('tag');
        if (tag) list = list.filter(c => (',' + (c.tags||'') + ',').toLowerCase().includes(',' + tag.toLowerCase() + ','));
        return { contacts: [...list].sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.updated_at||0)-new Date(a.updated_at||0)), total: ws.contacts.length };
      }
      if (method === 'POST') {
        if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');
        const c = { id: nextId(ws,'contact'), name: body.name, email: body.email||'', phone: body.phone||'', company: body.company||'', stage: body.stage||'lead', source: body.source||'manual', notes: body.notes||'', tags: body.tags||'', custom_fields: body.custom_fields||{}, ai_score: 0, ai_score_reason: '', created_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        ws.contacts.push(c); saveDB(); return c;
      }
    }
    const id = parseInt(parts[1]);
    const c = ws.contacts.find(x=>x.id===id);
    if (!c) throw new Error('Contact not found');
    if (method === 'GET') return { ...c, deals: ws.deals.filter(d=>d.contact_id===id).map(d=>({title:d.title,stage:d.stage,value:d.value})) };
    if (method === 'PATCH') { Object.assign(c, body); c.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); saveDB(); return c; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.contacts = ws.contacts.filter(x=>x.id!==id); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }

  // ── DEALS ──
  if (root === 'deals') {
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') return { deals: ws.deals.map(d=>withContactName(ws,d)) };
      if (method === 'POST') {
        if (!body.title) throw new Error('Title is required');
        const d = { id: nextId(ws,'deal'), title: body.title, contact_id: body.contact_id||null, value: body.value||0, stage: body.stage||'lead', probability: body.probability||20, close_date: body.close_date||'', notes: body.notes||'', created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        ws.deals.push(d); saveDB(); return d;
      }
    }
    const id = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const d = ws.deals.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!d) throw new Error('Deal not found'); Object.assign(d, body); saveDB(); return d; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.deals = ws.deals.filter(x=>x.id!==id); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }

  // ── TASKS ──
  if (root === 'tasks') {
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') return { tasks: ws.tasks.map(t=>withContactName(ws,t)).sort((a,b)=>(a.due_date||'9999').localeCompare(b.due_date||'9999')) };
      if (method === 'POST') {
        if (!body.title) throw new Error('Title is required');
        const t = { id: nextId(ws,'task'), title: body.title, description: body.description||'', priority: body.priority||'medium', due_date: body.due_date||'', status:'todo', contact_id: body.contact_id||null, created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        ws.tasks.push(t); saveDB(); return t;
      }
    }
    const id = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const t = ws.tasks.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!t) throw new Error('Task not found'); Object.assign(t, body); saveDB(); return t; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.tasks = ws.tasks.filter(x=>x.id!==id); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }

  // ── MESSAGES ──
  if (root === 'messages') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { messages: [...ws.messages].reverse().map(m=>withContactName(ws,m)) };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      const m = { id: nextId(ws,'message'), contact_id: body.contact_id||null, channel: body.channel||'email', subject: body.subject||'', body: body.body||'', ai_generated: body.ai_generated||0, created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.messages.push(m); saveDB(); return m;
    }
  }

  // ── APPOINTMENTS ──
  if (root === 'appointments') {
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') {
        const month = q.get('month');
        let list = ws.appointments;
        if (month) list = list.filter(a => a.date && a.date.startsWith(month));
        return { appointments: list.map(a=>withContactName(ws,a)).sort((a,b)=>(a.date+a.time).localeCompare(b.date+b.time)) };
      }
      if (method === 'POST') {
        if (!body.title || !body.date) throw new Error('Title and date required');
        const a = { id: nextId(ws,'appt'), title: body.title, date: body.date, time: body.time||'09:00', duration: body.duration||60, type: body.type||'call', notes: body.notes||'', contact_id: body.contact_id||null, status:'scheduled' };
        ws.appointments.push(a); saveDB(); return a;
      }
    }
    const id = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const a = ws.appointments.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!a) throw new Error('Appointment not found'); Object.assign(a, body); saveDB(); return a; }
  }

  // ── REVIEWS ──
  if (root === 'reviews') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { reviews: [...ws.reviews].reverse().map(r=>withContactName(ws,r)) };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      const r = { id: nextId(ws,'review'), platform: body.platform||'google', rating: body.rating||5, text: body.text||'', contact_id: null, ai_reply:'', status:'pending' };
      ws.reviews.push(r); saveDB(); return r;
    }
    const rid = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') {
      const r = ws.reviews.find(x=>x.id===rid); if (!r) throw new Error('Review not found');
      Object.assign(r, body); saveDB(); return r;
    }
  }

  // ── WORKFLOWS ──
  if (root === 'workflows') {
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') return { workflows: ws.workflows };
      if (method === 'POST') {
        if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');
        const w = { id: nextId(ws,'workflow'), name: body.name, trigger: body.trigger||'manual', status:'active', run_count:0, last_run:null, steps: body.steps||[], runs: [] };
        ws.workflows.push(w); saveDB(); return w;
      }
    }
    const id = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const w = ws.workflows.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!w) throw new Error('Workflow not found'); Object.assign(w, body); saveDB(); return w; }
  }

  // ── INVOICES ──
  if (root === 'invoices') {
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') return { invoices: ws.invoices.map(i=>withContactName(ws,i)) };
      if (method === 'POST') {
        const items = body.items||[];
        const subtotal = items.reduce((a,it)=>a+(it.qty*it.price),0);
        const tax = body.tax||0;
        const total = Math.round(subtotal * (1+tax/100) * 100)/100;
        const num = 'INV-' + (1000 + ws.invoices.length + 1);
        const inv = { id: nextId(ws,'invoice'), number:num, items, tax, total, status:'draft', due_date: body.due_date||'', contact_id: body.contact_id||null, paid_at:null, created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        ws.invoices.push(inv); saveDB(); return inv;
      }
    }
    const id = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const i = ws.invoices.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!i) throw new Error('Invoice not found'); Object.assign(i, body); if (body.status==='paid' && !i.paid_at) i.paid_at = new Date().toISOString(); saveDB(); return i; }
  }

  // ── SOCIAL ──
  if (root === 'social') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { posts: [...ws.social].reverse() };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      const p = { id: nextId(ws,'social'), platform: body.platform||'linkedin', content: body.content||'', status: body.status||'draft', ai_generated: body.ai_generated||0, created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.social.push(p); saveDB(); return p;
    }
    const pid = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const p = ws.social.find(x=>x.id===pid); if(!p) throw new Error('Post not found'); Object.assign(p, body); saveDB(); return p; }
  }

  // ── SUB-ACCOUNTS ──
  if (root === 'sub-accounts') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { accounts: ws.subAccounts };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');
      const a = { id: nextId(ws,'sub'), name: body.name, email: body.email||'', plan: body.plan||'starter', mrr: body.mrr||0, status:'active' };
      ws.subAccounts.push(a); saveDB(); return a;
    }
    const sid = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const a = ws.subAccounts.find(x=>x.id===sid); if(!a) throw new Error('Account not found'); Object.assign(a, body); saveDB(); return a; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.subAccounts = ws.subAccounts.filter(x=>x.id!==sid); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }

  // ── FORMS (local mode: full CRUD; public embed needs the backend) ──
  if (root === 'forms') {
    if (method === 'GET') {
      if (parts.length === 1) return { forms: [...ws.forms].reverse().map(f=>({...f, submissions: ws.formSubmissions.filter(x=>x.form_id===f.id).length})) };
      if (parts[2] === 'submissions') {
        const fid = parseInt(parts[1]);
        return { submissions: ws.formSubmissions.filter(x=>x.form_id===fid).reverse() };
      }
    }
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');
      const f = { id: nextId(ws,'form'), name: body.name, slug: 'local-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10), fields: body.fields||[], success_message: body.success_message||'Thanks!', active: 1, created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.forms.push(f); saveDB(); return { ...f, submissions: 0 };
    }
    const fid2 = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'DELETE' && parts[2] === 'submissions') { ws.formSubmissions = ws.formSubmissions.filter(x=>x.id!==parseInt(parts[3])); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const f = ws.forms.find(x=>x.id===fid2); if(!f) throw new Error('Form not found'); Object.assign(f, body); saveDB(); return f; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.forms = ws.forms.filter(x=>x.id!==fid2); ws.formSubmissions = ws.formSubmissions.filter(x=>x.form_id!==fid2); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }

  // ── COURSES / FUNNELS / AFFILIATES / COMMUNITY (local CRUD) ──
  if (root === 'courses') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { courses: [...ws.courses].reverse() };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.title) throw new Error('Title is required');
      const c = { id: nextId(ws,'course'), title: body.title, description: body.description||'', price: body.price||0, status: body.status||'draft', modules: body.modules||[], created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.courses.push(c); saveDB(); return c;
    }
    const cid = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const c = ws.courses.find(x=>x.id===cid); if(!c) throw new Error('Course not found'); Object.assign(c, body); saveDB(); return c; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.courses = ws.courses.filter(x=>x.id!==cid); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }
  if (root === 'funnels') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { funnels: [...ws.funnels].reverse() };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');
      const f = { id: nextId(ws,'funnel'), name: body.name, goal: body.goal||'', stages: body.stages||[], created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.funnels.push(f); saveDB(); return f;
    }
    const fid3 = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const f = ws.funnels.find(x=>x.id===fid3); if(!f) throw new Error('Funnel not found'); Object.assign(f, body); saveDB(); return f; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.funnels = ws.funnels.filter(x=>x.id!==fid3); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }
  if (root === 'affiliates') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { affiliates: [...ws.affiliates].reverse() };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');
      const a = { id: nextId(ws,'affiliate'), name: body.name, email: body.email||'', rate: body.rate||20, token: 'local-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,16), clicks: 0, conversions: 0, created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.affiliates.push(a); saveDB(); return a;
    }
    const aid = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const a = ws.affiliates.find(x=>x.id===aid); if(!a) throw new Error('Affiliate not found'); Object.assign(a, body); saveDB(); return a; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.affiliates = ws.affiliates.filter(x=>x.id!==aid); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }
  if (root === 'community') {
    if (method === 'GET') return { posts: [...ws.community].reverse() };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.title) throw new Error('Title is required');
      const p = { id: nextId(ws,'community'), title: body.title, content: body.content||'', created_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      ws.community.push(p); saveDB(); return p;
    }
    const pid2 = parseInt(parts[1]);
    if (method === 'PATCH') { const p = ws.community.find(x=>x.id===pid2); if(!p) throw new Error('Post not found'); Object.assign(p, body); saveDB(); return p; }
    if (method === 'DELETE') { ws.community = ws.community.filter(x=>x.id!==pid2); saveDB(); return { ok:true }; }
  }

  // ── SITES / TRIGGER LINKS / WEBCHAT (local mode: functional but not publicly hosted) ──
  if (root === 'sites') {
    if (!ws.sites) ws.sites = [];
    if (method === 'GET' && parts.length === 1) return { sites: ws.sites.map(s=>({...s, html_size:(s.html||'').length})) };
    if (method === 'POST') {
      if (!body.name) throw new Error('Name is required');

      { label: 'Next 30 days', days: 30, value: 0, count: 0 },
      { label: '31-60 days', days: 60, value: 0, count: 0 },
      { label: '61-90 days', days: 90, value: 0, count: 0 },
    ];

    for (const d of openD) {
      const value = Number(d.value||0);
      const prob = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (Number(d.probability)||0)/100));
      const weighted = value * prob;
      let b = 2;
      if (d.close_date && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d.close_date)) {
        const days = Math.floor((new Date(d.close_date+'T00:00:00Z') - now)/86400000);
        if (days <= 30) b = 0; else if (days <= 60) b = 1; else if (days <= 90) b = 2; else continue;
      } else {
        if (prob >= 0.8) b = 0; else if (prob >= 0.5) b = 1;
      }
      buckets[b].value += weighted; buckets[b].count += 1;
    }

      else reasons.push('Average deal size is small');
      const stale = ws.deals.filter(d=>!['won','lost'].includes(d.stage) && d.created_at && (Date.now()-new Date(d.created_at).getTime())/86400000 > 30).length;
      if (stale > 0) { score -= Math.min(12, stale*4); reasons.push(`${stale} deal(s) untouched for 30+ days`); }
    }
    if (st.pending_tasks > 0) { score -= Math.min(10, st.pending_tasks*2); reasons.push(`${st.pending_tasks} open task(s)`); }
    else { score += 5; reasons.push('No overdue tasks'); }
    if (st.won_revenue > 0) { score += 7; reasons.push(`Won $${st.won_revenue.toLocaleString()} so far`); }
    score = Math.max(5, Math.min(99, Math.round(score)));
    return { score, verdict: score>=80?'Excellent':score>=60?'Healthy':score>=40?'Needs attention':'Critical', reasons };
  }
  if (rawPath === '/ai/analyze-image' && method === 'POST') {
    throw new Error('Image analysis needs the deployed backend + a vision model (e.g. meta/llama-3.2-90b-vision-instruct on NVIDIA).');
  }
  if (rawPath === '/ai/usage') return { ...ws.aiUsage, tokens_today: ws.aiUsage.tokens_today||0, by_op: [] };
  if (rawPath === '/ai/health') return aiHealth(ws);
  if (rawPath === '/ai/insights/dashboard') return { insights: aiInsightsDashboard(ws) };
  if (rawPath === '/ai/complete' && method==='POST') return aiOpComplete(ws, body.prompt, body);
  if (rawPath === '/ai/generate' && method==='POST') return aiOpGenerate(ws, body);
  if (rawPath === '/ai/sentiment' && method==='POST') return aiOpSentiment(ws, body.text);
  if (root === 'ai' && parts[1] === 'score-lead') {
    const id = parseInt(parts[2]);
    const c = ws.contacts.find(x=>x.id===id);
    if (!c) throw new Error('Contact not found');
    const r = await aiOpScoreLead(ws, c);
    c.ai_score = r.score; c.ai_score_reason = r.reason; c.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();
    saveDB();
    return r;
  }
  if (rawPath === '/ai/build-workflow' && method==='POST') { const workflow = await aiOpBuildWorkflow(ws, body.goal); return { workflow }; }

  throw new Error(`No local handler for ${method} ${rawPath}`);
}

// ── UNIFIED api() — real backend if configured/detected, else local engine ──
async function realFetch(path, method, body) {
  const opts = { method, headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', ...(STATE.token?{Authorization:`Bearer ${STATE.token}`}:{}) } };
  if (body) opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
  let r;
  try { r = await fetch(API + path, opts); }
  catch (e) {
    // Network failure — never silently pretend local mode is the backend.
    if (REAL_MODE()) throw new Error('Backend unreachable — check your connection and Settings → System → Backend URL.');
    throw e;
  }
  if (r.status === 401) {
    // On the login endpoint a 401 means "wrong credentials" — surface it.
    if (path === '/auth/login' || path === '/auth/register') {
      const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(d.error || 'Invalid email or password');
    }
    doLogout();
    return null;
  }
  // A real API always answers JSON. If something answers 200 with HTML or
  // any other non-JSON body (the exact failure mode of the bundled local
  // static server being mistaken for a real backend — see pingBackend()),
  // treat it as an error instead of silently returning `{}` as if it were
  // valid, empty-but-successful data.
  const contentType = r.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  if (!contentType.includes('application/json')) {
    BACKEND.available = false; // this clearly isn't a real API — stop trusting it for the rest of the session
    throw new Error(`Got a non-JSON response from ${API}${path} (content-type: ${contentType || 'none'}) — this usually means the configured Backend URL isn't a real deployed backend. Check Settings → System.`);
  }
  let data;
  try { data = await r.json(); }
  catch { throw new Error(`Got an unreadable response from ${API}${path} — check Settings → System → Backend URL.`); }
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(data.error || `Server error ${r.status}`);
  return data;
}
async function api(path, method='GET', body=null) {
  if (REAL_MODE()) return realFetch(path, method, body);
  if (BACKEND.available === null) await pingBackend();
  if (BACKEND.available) {
    try { return await realFetch(path, method, body); }
    catch(e) {
      if (e instanceof TypeError) { await pingBackend(); if (!BACKEND.available) return localApi(path, method, body); }
      throw e;
    }
  }
  return localApi(path, method, body);
}


// ── WHATSAPP PHONE HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────
// wa.me links need FULL international format (country code, no leading 0,
// no spaces/dashes/+). This is the #1 reason "click to WhatsApp" silently
// opens a broken chat, so every wa.me link in the app should go through
// waPhone()/waLink() rather than rolling its own .replace(/\D/g,'').
function waPhone(raw) {
  return (raw || '').replace(/\D/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '');
}
// Heuristic only — there's no way to truly validate a phone number without
// calling a carrier API, but this catches the most common mistake: entering
// a local number with no country code at all (e.g. "0790 123 456").
function phoneLooksIntl(raw) {
  const digits = waPhone(raw);
  return digits.length >= 8 && (raw || '').trim().startsWith('+') || digits.length >= 11;
}
function waLink(phone, text) {
  const p = waPhone(phone);
  return `https://wa.me/${p}${text ? '?text=' + encodeURIComponent(text) : ''}`;
}

// ── UTILITY ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
const $$ = n => n == null ? '—' : Number(n).toLocaleString();
const $$$ = n => n == null ? '—' : '$' + Number(n).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits:0, maximumFractionDigits:0});
const V = id => document.getElementById(id);
// Tolerates ISO-8601 (2026-08-22T10:00:00.000Z) AND SQLite-style
// (2026-08-22 10:00:00) timestamps — Safari can't parse the latter natively.
function parseDate(d) {
  if (!d) return null;
  let t;
  if (typeof d === 'string' && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}[ T]/.test(d) && !d.includes('T')) t = new Date(d.replace(' ', 'T') + 'Z');
  else t = new Date(d);
  return isNaN(t.getTime()) ? null : t;
}
const timeAgo = d => {
  const t = parseDate(d);
  if (!t) return '—';
  const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - t.getTime()) / 1000);
  if (s < 60) return 'just now';
  if (s < 3600) return Math.floor(s/60) + 'm ago';
  if (s < 86400) return Math.floor(s/3600) + 'h ago';
  return Math.floor(s/86400) + 'd ago';
};
// ── OUTPUT ESCAPING (XSS protection) ─────────────────────────
// Every user/email-sourced value rendered into innerHTML or an onclick
// attribute MUST go through esc() / escAttr(). Email subject lines,
// sender names, contact fields and AI output are all attacker-controlled.
function esc(s) {
  return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
}
function escAttr(s) { return esc(s); }
// Sanitize an HTML email body: strips scripts, event handlers, javascript:
// URLs and embedded objects — the rest renders safely.
function sanitizeHtml(h) {
  if (!h) return '';
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(String(h), 'text/html');
  doc.querySelectorAll('script,style,iframe,object,embed,link,meta,form').forEach(n => n.remove());
  doc.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(n => {
    [...n.attributes].forEach(a => {
      const name = a.name.toLowerCase();
      const val = a.value.toLowerCase();
      if (name.startsWith('on') || (a.name === 'href' && /^\s*javascript:/i.test(val)) || (a.name === 'src' && /^\s*javascript:/i.test(val))) n.removeAttribute(a.name);
    });
  });
  return doc.body ? doc.body.innerHTML : '';
}
function downloadCSV(filename, rows) {
  const csv = rows.map(r => r.map(v => { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v); return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s; }).join(',')).join('\n');
  const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
}
// Proper CSV line parser (handles quoted fields with commas/newlines).
function parseCSVLine(line) {
  const out = []; let cur = ''; let inQ = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
    const ch = line[i];
    if (inQ) {
      if (ch === '"' && line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
      else if (ch === '"') inQ = false;
      else cur += ch;
    } else {
      if (ch === '"') inQ = true;
      else if (ch === ',') { out.push(cur); cur = ''; }
      else cur += ch;
    }
  }
  out.push(cur);
  return out;
}
const stageBadge = s => {
  const m = {lead:'blue',prospect:'yellow',qualified:'purple',proposal:'orange',negotiation:'orange',won:'green',lost:'red',customer:'green',churned:'red'};
  return `<span class="badge badge-${m[s]||'gray'}">${s||'—'}</span>`;
};
const priorityBadge = p => {
  const m = {urgent:'red',high:'orange',medium:'yellow',low:'gray'};
  return `<span class="badge badge-${m[p]||'gray'}">${p||'medium'}</span>`;
};
const aiScoreBadge = score => {
  if (!score) return '<span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:11px">—</span>';
  const cls = score>=70?'hot':score>=40?'warm':'cold';
  const icon = score>=70?'🔥':score>=40?'♨️':'❄️';
  return `<span class="ai-score ${cls}">${icon} ${score}</span>`;
};
const priorityDot = p => ({high:'🔴',medium:'🟡',low:'🟢'}[p]||'⚪');
const getGreeting = () => { const h=new Date().getHours(); return h<12?'morning':h<17?'afternoon':'evening'; };
function debounce(fn, ms) { let t; return (...a)=>{ clearTimeout(t); t=setTimeout(()=>fn(...a),ms); }; }
function openShortcutsModal() {
  openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">⌨️ Keyboard Shortcuts</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      ${[['Ctrl + J','Toggle AI assistant panel'],['Ctrl + K','Focus global search'],['Ctrl + Enter','Send chat message'],['Ctrl + /','This help'],['Esc','Close modal / panels'],['/task …','AI Agent: create a task'],['/contact …','AI Agent: add a contact'],['/deal …','AI Agent: add a deal'],['/forecast','AI Agent: sales forecast'],['/weekly','AI Agent: weekly review']].map(([k,d])=>`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-size:13px"><span style="color:var(--text2)">${esc(d)}</span><code style="background:var(--card2);padding:2px 8px;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px">${esc(k)}</code></div>`).join('')}
    </div>`);
}

// ── API HELPER ────────────────────────────────────────────────

// ── WEBSOCKET ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Optional, best-effort real-time push (a handful of toast alerts). The
// reference Cloudflare Workers backend (backend/DEPLOY.md) doesn't
// implement this endpoint on purpose — it's low value relative to the
// added complexity (Durable Objects, a new billing dimension) for a
// solo-user CRM, and everything else works fine without it. Rather than
// retrying forever against an endpoint that will never answer, this
// backs off and gives up quietly after a few tries per session.
let ws, wsFailCount = 0;
const WS_MAX_RETRIES = 4;
function connectWS() {
  if (!STATE.token || wsFailCount >= WS_MAX_RETRIES) return;
  try {
    ws = new WebSocket(WS_URL);
    ws.onopen = () => { console.log('WS connected'); wsFailCount = 0; };
    ws.onmessage = e => {
      try {
        const d = JSON.parse(e.data);
        if (d.type === 'alert') toast(d.message, 'error');
        if (d.type === 'info') toast(d.message, 'info');
        if (d.type === 'connected') updateAIStatus('ok');
      } catch {}
    };
    ws.onclose = () => { wsFailCount++; if (wsFailCount < WS_MAX_RETRIES) setTimeout(connectWS, 4000); };
    ws.onerror = () => ws.close();
  } catch { wsFailCount++; if (wsFailCount < WS_MAX_RETRIES) setTimeout(connectWS, 4000); }
}

// ── TOAST ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function toast(msg, type='info', duration=3800) {
  const icons = { success:'✅', error:'❌', info:'ℹ️', warning:'⚠️' };
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = `toast ${type}`;
  el.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size:16px">${icons[type]||'💬'}</span><span style="flex:1">${esc(msg)}</span><span onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" style="cursor:pointer;opacity:.5;margin-left:4px">×</span>`;
  V('toast-container').appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => { el.style.opacity='0'; el.style.transform='translateX(20px)'; el.style.transition='.3s'; setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),300); }, duration);
  return el;
}

// ── MODAL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function openModal(html, cls='') {
  closeModal();
  const overlay = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.className = 'modal-overlay';
  overlay.id = 'modal-overlay';
  overlay.innerHTML = `<div class="modal ${cls}">${html}</div>`;
  overlay.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === overlay) closeModal(); });
  V('modal-container').appendChild(overlay);
}
function closeModal() { V('modal-overlay')?.remove(); }

// ── ROUTER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const VIEW_TITLES = {
  dashboard:'Dashboard', 'ai-hub':'🧠 AI Command Hub',
  gmail:'📧 Gmail Inbox', inbox:'Messages', contacts:'Contacts',
  pipeline:'Pipeline', tasks:'Tasks', whatsapp:'WhatsApp Outreach',
  workflows:'Automations', calendar:'Calendar', reviews:'Reviews',
  social:'Social Media', funnels:'Funnels', forms:'Forms', websites:'🌐 Websites', webchat:'💬 Webchat Widget',
  invoices:'Invoices', courses:'Courses', community:'Community',
  affiliates:'Affiliates', 'ai-bots':'AI Bots',
  'content-ai':'Content AI Studio', 'voice-ai':'Voice AI',
  'sub-accounts':'Sub-Accounts', saas:'SaaS Mode',
  reports:'Reports & Analytics', settings:'Settings'
};

// Renders an in-page recoverable error instead of leaving the user stuck
// on an infinite spinner or a blank screen when a view throws.
function renderViewError(view, err) {
  console.error(`View "${view}" failed:`, err);
  content(`<div class="empty-state">
    <div class="empty-icon">⚠️</div>
    <div class="empty-title">This screen hit a snag</div>
    <div class="empty-text" style="max-width:420px;margin:0 auto 16px">${(err?.message || String(err)).replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="navigate('${view}')">🔄 Retry</button>
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="navigate('dashboard')">🏠 Dashboard</button>
    </div>
  </div>`);
}
function navigate(view) {
  STATE.view = view;
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item[data-view]').forEach(n => n.classList.toggle('active', n.dataset.view === view));
  V('topbar-title').textContent = VIEW_TITLES[view] || view;
  V('content').innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading...</div>';
  const fn = views[view];
  if (!fn) { content(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">🚧</div><div class="empty-title">Coming soon</div></div>`); return; }
  try {
    const result = fn();
    // Views can be async (return a Promise) or sync — catch failures either way
    // so a bug in one screen never strands the user on a dead spinner.
    if (result && typeof result.catch === 'function') result.catch(e => renderViewError(view, e));
  } catch (e) { renderViewError(view, e); }
}
function content(html) { V('content').innerHTML = html; }

// ── GLOBAL SAFETY NET ──────────────────────────────────────────
// Catches anything that slips past individual try/catch blocks (stray
// event-handler exceptions, unhandled promise rejections) and surfaces it
// as a toast instead of failing silently or white-screening the app.
window.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
  console.error('Uncaught error:', e.error || e.message);
  if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Something went wrong — check the console for details.', 'error', 4000);
});
window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (e) => {
  console.error('Unhandled promise rejection:', e.reason);
  if (typeof toast === 'function') toast((e.reason && e.reason.message) || 'A background action failed.', 'error', 4000);
});

// ── AUTH ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function switchAuthTab(tab) {
  V('auth-login').style.display = tab === 'login' ? '' : 'none';
  V('auth-register').style.display = tab === 'register' ? '' : 'none';
  document.querySelectorAll('.auth-tab').forEach((t,i) => t.classList.toggle('active', (i===0&&tab==='login')||(i===1&&tab==='register')));
}
async function doLogin() {
  const email = V('login-email').value.trim();
  const password = V('login-password').value;
  if (!email || !password) { toast('Enter email and password','error'); return; }
  try {
    const d = await api('/auth/login','POST',{ email, password });
    if (d) loginSuccess(d);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message || 'Login failed','error'); }
}
async function doRegister() {
  const name = V('reg-name').value.trim();
  const email = V('reg-email').value.trim();
  const password = V('reg-password').value;
  if (!name || !email || !password) { toast('All fields required','error'); return; }
  if (password.length < 8) { toast('Password must be at least 8 characters','error'); return; }
  try {
    const d = await api('/auth/register','POST',{ name, email, password });
    if (d) loginSuccess(d);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message || 'Registration failed','error'); }
}
async function doDemo() {
  try {
    const d = await api('/auth/demo','POST',{});
    if (d) { loginSuccess(d); toast('Starting fresh — no sample data, just an empty workspace.','info',5000); }
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message || 'Could not start — please try again.','error',6000); }
}
function loginSuccess(d) {
  STATE.token = d.token;
  STATE.user = d.user;
  localStorage.setItem('nx_token', d.token);
  localStorage.setItem('nx_user', JSON.stringify(d.user));
  V('auth-screen').style.display = 'none';
  V('user-display-name').textContent = d.user.name;
  V('user-avatar').textContent = (d.user.name || 'U')[0].toUpperCase();
  connectWS();
  loadAISettings();
  checkGmailConnection();
  navigate('dashboard');
}
function doLogout() {
  STATE.token = null; STATE.user = null; STATE.aiSettings = null;
  localStorage.removeItem('nx_token'); localStorage.removeItem('nx_user');
  try { if (ws) { ws.onclose = null; ws.close(); } } catch {}
  ws = null;
  V('auth-screen').style.display = 'flex';
  V('user-display-name').textContent = 'Loading...';
}

// ── AI SETTINGS ───────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadAISettings() {
  try {
    const s = await api('/ai/settings');
    STATE.aiSettings = s;
    const hasKey = (s.provider==='nvidia'&&s.nvidia_key_set)||(s.provider==='openai'&&s.openai_key_set)||(s.provider==='custom');
    const provName = s.provider==='nvidia'?'NVIDIA NIM':s.provider==='custom'?'Custom AI':'OpenAI';
    V('panel-model-label').textContent = `${provName} • ${s.model||'Default'}`;
    if (!hasKey) { updateAIStatus('none'); }
    else if (STATE.aiVerified && STATE.aiVerified.provider === s.provider && STATE.aiVerified.status === 'ok') { updateAIStatus('ok'); }
    else { updateAIStatus('unverified'); }
  } catch {}
}
// state: 'none' (no key) | 'unverified' (key saved, not tested this session) | 'ok' (tested and working) | 'error' (tested and failed)
function updateAIStatus(state) {
  const dot = V('ai-dot'); const txt = V('ai-status-text');
  if (!dot) return;
  if (state === 'ok') { dot.style.background='var(--green)'; dot.style.boxShadow='0 0 6px var(--green)'; txt.textContent='AI Ready'; }
  else if (state === 'unverified') { dot.style.background='var(--blue,#3b82f6)'; dot.style.boxShadow='none'; txt.textContent='Key Saved — Untested'; }
  else if (state === 'error') { dot.style.background='var(--red)'; dot.style.boxShadow='0 0 6px var(--red)'; txt.textContent='Connection Error'; }
  else { dot.style.background='var(--yellow)'; dot.style.boxShadow='none'; txt.textContent='No AI Key'; }
}

    addPanelMsg('assistant', '👋 Hi! I\'m your NexusCRM AI. I can:\n\n• Answer questions about your contacts, deals & tasks\n• Draft emails, WhatsApp messages & social posts\n• Analyze your pipeline and give sales advice\n• Build automations\n• Summarize and translate content\n\nWhat can I help with?');
  }
  if (STATE.panelOpen) setTimeout(() => V('panel-input')?.focus(), 150);
}

let lastPanelMeta = null;
async function rateAIReply(rating, provider, model) {
  try {
    await api('/ai/feedback','POST',{ rating, provider, model, op: 'chat' });
    toast(rating === 1 ? 'Thanks — glad it helped! 💜' : "Thanks — we'll use that to improve.",'success',2500);
