function addPanelMsg(role, text, pushHistory=true, meta=null) {
  if (pushHistory) STATE.panelMessages.push({ role, content: text });
  const msgs = V('panel-messages');
  const t = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = `chat-msg ${role}`;
  const av = role==='user' ? `<div class="msg-avatar user-av">👤</div>` : `<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">🧠</div>`;
  const metaLine = (meta && role === 'assistant') ? `<div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:2px">via ${esc(meta.provider||'AI')} • ${esc(meta.model||'')}</div>` : '';
  const fb = (role === 'assistant') ? `<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:3px"><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="padding:1px 7px;font-size:10px" onclick="rateAIReply(1,'${escAttr(meta?.provider||'')}','${escAttr(meta?.model||'')}')" title="Helpful">👍</button><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="padding:1px 7px;font-size:10px" onclick="rateAIReply(-1,'${escAttr(meta?.provider||'')}','${escAttr(meta?.model||'')}')" title="Not helpful">👎</button></div>` : '';
  div.innerHTML = `${av}<div><div class="msg-bubble">${esc(text).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div>${metaLine}${fb}<div class="msg-time">${t}</div></div>`;
  msgs.appendChild(div);
  msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight;
  return div;
}
async function sendPanelMessage() {
  const input = V('panel-input');
  const text = input.value.trim();
  if (!text) return;
  input.value = ''; input.style.height = '';
  addPanelMsg('user', text);
  const msgs = V('panel-messages');
  const typing = document.createElement('div');
  typing.className = 'chat-msg assistant';
  typing.innerHTML = '<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">🧠</div><div class="chat-typing"><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div></div>';
  msgs.appendChild(typing); msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight;
  try {
    // Agent mode: messages starting with "/" are CRM commands the AI executes.
    if (text.startsWith('/')) {
      try {
        const agentRes = await api('/ai/agent', 'POST', { message: text.slice(1) });
        typing.remove();
        const badge = agentRes.action && agentRes.action !== 'none'
          ? `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--green);font-weight:700;margin-top:4px">✅ ${esc(agentRes.action.replace(/_/g,' '))}</div>` : '';
        addPanelMsg('assistant', (agentRes.reply || 'Done!') + badge);
      } catch (e) { typing.remove(); addPanelMsg('assistant', '⚠️ ' + e.message); }
      return;
    }
    const ctx = `User is on the ${STATE.view} page. User: ${STATE.user?.name}.`;
    const history = STATE.panelMessages.slice(-10);
    const res = await chatStreamFetch(history, ctx);
    typing.remove();
    if (!res.ok) {
      // Non-stream error (cap reached, provider down, auth…) — show it, don't hang.
      let msg = 'AI request failed';
      try { msg = (await res.json()).error || msg; } catch {}
      addPanelMsg('assistant', '⚠️ ' + msg);
      return;
    }
    const aiDiv = addPanelMsg('assistant', '', false); // display-only; no history entry yet
    const bubble = aiDiv.querySelector('.msg-bubble');
    bubble.classList.add('streaming-text');
    const reader = res.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let full = '';
    let failed = false;
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      for (const line of decoder.decode(value).split('\n')) {
        if (!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue;
        try {
          const d = JSON.parse(line.slice(6));
          if (d.meta) lastPanelMeta = d.meta;
          if (d.delta) { full += d.delta; bubble.innerHTML = esc(full).replace(/\n/g,'<br>'); msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight; }
          if (d.done) { bubble.classList.remove('streaming-text'); }
          if (d.error) { failed = true; bubble.textContent = '⚠️ ' + d.error; bubble.classList.remove('streaming-text'); }
        } catch {}
      }
    }
    if (failed) return;
    // Replace the placeholder history entry (no duplicate empty messages).
    const last = STATE.panelMessages[STATE.panelMessages.length - 1];
    if (last && last.role === 'assistant' && last.content === '') STATE.panelMessages[STATE.panelMessages.length - 1] = { role: 'assistant', content: full };
    else STATE.panelMessages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: full });
    if (lastPanelMeta) {
      const metaDiv = document.createElement('div');
      metaDiv.style.cssText = 'font-size:9px;color:var(--text3);text-align:right;margin-top:-4px';
      metaDiv.textContent = `via ${lastPanelMeta.provider||'AI'} • ${lastPanelMeta.model||''}`;
      msgs.appendChild(metaDiv);
    }
  } catch(e) {
    typing.remove();
    addPanelMsg('assistant', '⚠️ AI unavailable. Check Settings → AI Providers.');
  }
}
function panelKeydown(e) { if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();sendPanelMessage();} }
function autoResize(el) { el.style.height=''; el.style.height=Math.min(el.scrollHeight,120)+'px'; }
function handleGlobalSearch(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') { navigate('contacts'); setTimeout(()=>views.contacts(e.target.value),100); }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// GMAIL INTEGRATION
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const GMAIL_CLIENT_ID = localStorage.getItem('nx_gmail_client_id') || '';
const GMAIL_SCOPES = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.readonly https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.send https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.modify';

async function checkGmailConnection() {
  const token = STATE.gmailToken;
  if (!token) { return; }
  try {
    // verify token
    const r = await fetch('https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/profile', {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
    });
    if (r.ok) {
      const p = await r.json();
      STATE.gmailConnected = true;
      STATE.gmailEmail = p.emailAddress;
      V('gmail-topbar-btn').style.display = 'flex';
      updateGmailBadge();
      toast(`Gmail connected: ${p.emailAddress}`, 'success');
    } else {
      STATE.gmailToken = null;
      localStorage.removeItem('nx_gmail_token');
    }
  } catch { STATE.gmailToken = null; }
}

function connectGmail() {
  const clientId = localStorage.getItem('nx_gmail_client_id');
  if (!clientId) {
    openModal(`
      <div class="modal-header">
        <div class="modal-title">📧 Connect Gmail</div>
        <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button>
      </div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <div class="ai-insight" style="margin-bottom:16px">
          <div class="ai-insight-title">Free Gmail OAuth Setup</div>
          <div class="ai-insight-text">To use real Gmail, you need a free Google OAuth Client ID. This is 100% free and gives you real email sending & receiving.</div>
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:16px">
          <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px;">Setup steps (5 minutes):</div>
          <div>1. Go to <a href="https://console.cloud.google.com" target="_blank" style="color:var(--accent)">console.cloud.google.com</a></div>
          <div>2. Create a new project → Enable <strong>Gmail API</strong></div>
          <div>3. Go to <strong>Credentials</strong> → Create <strong>OAuth 2.0 Client ID</strong></div>
          <div>4. App type: <strong>Web Application</strong></div>
          <div>5. Add <strong>both</strong> of these (Google rejects the login if the redirect URI is missing):</div>
          <div>&nbsp;&nbsp;• Authorized JavaScript origin: <code style="background:var(--card2);padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px">${window.location.origin}</code></div>
          <div>&nbsp;&nbsp;• Authorized redirect URI: <code style="background:var(--card2);padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px">${window.location.origin}/oauth-callback</code></div>
          <div>6. Copy your Client ID and paste below</div>
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
          <label>Google OAuth Client ID</label>
          <input id="gmail-client-id-input" placeholder="123456789-abc...apps.googleusercontent.com">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="modal-footer">
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
        <button class="btn btn-gmail" onclick="saveGmailClientId()">Save & Connect</button>
      </div>
    `);
    return;
  }
  startGmailOAuth(clientId);
}

function saveGmailClientId() {
  const id = V('gmail-client-id-input')?.value?.trim();
  if (!id) { toast('Enter your Client ID','error'); return; }
  localStorage.setItem('nx_gmail_client_id', id);
  closeModal();
  startGmailOAuth(id);
}

function startGmailOAuth(clientId) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    client_id: clientId,
    redirect_uri: window.location.origin + '/oauth-callback',
    response_type: 'token',
    scope: GMAIL_SCOPES,
    include_granted_scopes: 'true',
  });
  // Use popup approach. The popup redirects back to this app's origin, so
  // it is same-origin and we can read its hash directly — but the app's own
  // init() also strips the hash, so the popup ALSO posts the token via
  // postMessage. Whichever arrives first wins.
  const popup = window.open(`https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/auth?${params}`, 'gmail-oauth', 'width=500,height=600');
  let done = false;
  const finish = (token) => {
    if (done || !token) return;
    done = true;
    clearInterval(check);
    window.removeEventListener('message', onMsg);
    STATE.gmailToken = token;
    localStorage.setItem('nx_gmail_token', token);
    try { popup && !popup.closed && popup.close(); } catch {}
    checkGmailConnection();
  };
  const onMsg = (e) => {
    if (e.data && e.data.type === 'gmail-token' && e.data.token) finish(e.data.token);
  };
  window.addEventListener('message', onMsg);
  const check = setInterval(() => {
    try {
      if (popup.closed) { clearInterval(check); window.removeEventListener('message', onMsg); return; }
      const hash = popup.location.hash;
      if (hash && hash.includes('access_token')) {
        const p = new URLSearchParams(hash.slice(1));
        finish(p.get('access_token'));
      }
    } catch {}
  }, 300);
}

function toggleGmailPanel() {
  STATE.gmailPanelOpen = !STATE.gmailPanelOpen;
  V('gmail-panel').classList.toggle('visible', STATE.gmailPanelOpen);
  if (STATE.gmailPanelOpen) loadGmailInbox();
}

async function loadGmailInbox() {
  if (!STATE.gmailToken) { V('gmail-panel-status').textContent = 'Not connected'; return; }
  V('gmail-panel-list').innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
  try {
    const r = await fetch('https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages?maxResults=20&q=in:inbox', {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}` }
    });
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Gmail token expired');
    const data = await r.json();
    const messages = data.messages || [];
    // fetch details for first 10
    const details = await Promise.all(
      messages.slice(0,10).map(m =>
        fetch(`https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/${m.id}?format=metadata&metadataHeaders=From&metadataHeaders=Subject&metadataHeaders=Date`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}` }
        }).then(r=>r.json())
      )
    );
    STATE.gmailEmails = details;
    const unread = details.filter(m => m.labelIds?.includes('UNREAD')).length;
    V('gmail-panel-status').textContent = `${details.length} emails • ${unread} unread`;
    updateGmailBadge(unread);
    renderGmailList(details);
  } catch(e) {
    V('gmail-panel-list').innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">⚠️</div><div class="empty-text">${e.message}</div><button class="btn btn-gmail btn-sm" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="connectGmail()">Reconnect Gmail</button></div>`;
  }
}

function renderGmailList(emails) {
  const list = V('gmail-panel-list');
  if (!emails.length) { list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-text">No emails found</div></div>'; return; }
  list.innerHTML = emails.map(m => {
    const hdrs = {};
    (m.payload?.headers || []).forEach(h => hdrs[h.name] = h.value);
    const from = esc((hdrs.From || 'Unknown').replace(/<[^>]+>/,'').trim());
    const subj = esc(hdrs.Subject || '(No subject)');
    const date = hdrs.Date ? new Date(hdrs.Date).toLocaleDateString([],{month:'short',day:'numeric'}) : '';
    const unread = m.labelIds?.includes('UNREAD');
    return `<div class="gmail-email-row ${unread?'unread':''}" onclick="openEmail('${m.id}')">
      <div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${unread?'var(--accent)':'transparent'};flex-shrink:0"></div>
      <div class="gmail-from">${from}</div>
      <div class="gmail-subject">${subj}</div>
      <div class="gmail-time">${date}</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

async function openEmail(id) {
  try {
    const r = await fetch(`https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/${id}?format=full`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}` }
    });
    const m = await r.json();
    const hdrs = {};
    (m.payload?.headers || []).forEach(h => hdrs[h.name] = h.value);
    const from = hdrs.From || 'Unknown';
    const subj = hdrs.Subject || '(No subject)';
    const date = hdrs.Date || '';
    STATE.currentGmailThreadId = m.threadId || id;
    let body = '';
    const findBody = part => {
      if (!part) return;
      if (part.body?.data) {
        const text = atob(part.body.data.replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/'));
        if (part.mimeType?.includes('html')) body = sanitizeHtml(text);
        else if (!body) body = `<pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px">${esc(text)}</pre>`;
      }
      if (part.parts) part.parts.forEach(findBody);
    };
    findBody(m.payload);
    // mark as read
    fetch(`https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/${id}/modify`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ removeLabelIds: ['UNREAD'] })
    }).catch(()=>{});
    openModal(`
      <div class="modal-header">
        <div>
          <div class="modal-title" style="font-size:15px">${esc(subj)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);margin-top:2px">From: ${esc(from)} &nbsp;•&nbsp; ${new Date(date).toLocaleString()}</div>
        </div>
        <button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button>
      </div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:14px">
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="replyToEmail('${escAttr(from)}','${escAttr(subj)}','${id}')">↩ Reply</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiReplyEmail('${escAttr(from)}','${escAttr(subj)}','${id}')">🤖 AI Reply</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="saveEmailToInbox('${id}','${escAttr(from)}','${escAttr(subj)}')">💾 Save to CRM</button>
        </div>
        <div style="background:var(--card2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;max-height:400px;overflow-y:auto;font-size:13px;line-height:1.6">
          ${body || '<em style="color:var(--text3)">Could not render email body</em>'}
        </div>
      </div>`, 'modal-xl');
  } catch(e) { toast('Could not load email: ' + e.message, 'error'); }
}

function replyToEmail(from, subject, id) {
  const replyTo = from.match(/<(.+)>/)?.[1] || from;
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">↩ Reply</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>To</label><input id="reply-to" value="${escAttr(replyTo)}"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Subject</label><input id="reply-subject" value="Re: ${escAttr(subject)}"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Message</label><textarea id="reply-body" rows="8" placeholder="Type your reply..."></textarea></div>
      <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiDraftReply('${escAttr(from)}')" style="margin-bottom:8px">🤖 AI Draft Reply</button>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-gmail" onclick="sendGmailReply('${id}')">📧 Send Reply</button>
    </div>`);
}

async function aiDraftReply(from) {
  toast('AI drafting reply...','info');
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'email',tone:'professional',context:`Reply to: ${from}. Keep it professional and helpful.`});
    if (V('reply-body')) V('reply-body').value = r.content;
    toast('AI reply drafted!','success');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

async function sendGmailReply(threadId) {
  const to = V('reply-to')?.value;
  const subject = V('reply-subject')?.value;
  const body = V('reply-body')?.value;
  if (!to || !body) { toast('Fill in To and Message fields','error'); return; }
  const thread = STATE.currentGmailThreadId || threadId;
  try {
    // Build RFC 2822 email
    const email = [
      `To: ${to}`,
      `Subject: ${subject}`,
      'Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8',
      'MIME-Version: 1.0',
      '',
      body
    ].join('\r\n');
    const encoded = btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(email))).replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_').replace(/=+$/,'');
    const r = await fetch('https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/send', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ raw: encoded, threadId: thread })
    });
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Send failed');
    // Save to CRM messages
    await api('/messages','POST',{ channel:'email', subject, body, direction:'outbound' }).catch(()=>{});
    closeModal();
    toast('Reply sent via Gmail! ✅','success');
  } catch(e) { toast('Gmail send failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}

async function aiReplyEmail(from, subject, id) {
  toast('AI generating reply...','info');
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'email',context:`Professional reply to email from ${from} about: ${subject}`});
    replyToEmail(from, subject, id);
    setTimeout(() => { if(V('reply-body')) V('reply-body').value = r.content; }, 200);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

async function saveEmailToInbox(id, from, subject) {
  try {
    await api('/messages','POST',{ channel:'email', subject, body:`Email from ${from} (imported from Gmail)`, direction:'inbound' });
    toast('Saved to CRM Messages!','success');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

function updateGmailBadge(count) {
  const badge = V('gmail-unread-badge');
  if (!badge) return;
  if (count && count > 0) { badge.textContent = count; badge.style.display=''; }
  else badge.style.display='none';
}

function openGmailCompose() {
  if (!STATE.gmailToken) { connectGmail(); return; }
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">✉️ New Email</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>To *</label><input id="compose-to" placeholder="recipient@email.com" type="email"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Subject</label><input id="compose-subject" placeholder="Email subject"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Message</label><textarea id="compose-body" rows="8" placeholder="Write your email..."></textarea></div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiComposeEmail()">🤖 AI Write Email</button><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="insertSnippetIntoCompose()">📌 Insert Snippet</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-gmail" onclick="sendGmailCompose()">📧 Send via Gmail</button>
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="sendViaBackend()">📨 Send via Server</button>
    </div>`, 'modal-lg');
}

function insertSnippetIntoCompose() {
  const snips = getSnippets();
  if (!snips.length) { toast('No snippets saved yet — save some AI outputs first!','info'); return; }
  openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📌 Insert Snippet</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body"><div style="max-height:360px;overflow-y:auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
      ${snips.slice(0,10).map((x,i)=>`<div class="card card-sm" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="pickSnippet(${i})"><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);white-space:pre-wrap;max-height:70px;overflow:hidden">${esc(x.text.slice(0,200))}${x.text.length>200?'…':''}</div></div>`).join('')}
    </div></div>`,'modal-lg');
  window.__snippetPick = snips;
}
function pickSnippet(i) {
  const t = (window.__snippetPick||[])[i]?.text;
  if (t && V('compose-body')) V('compose-body').value = (V('compose-body').value ? V('compose-body').value + '\n\n' : '') + t;
  closeModal();
}
async function aiComposeEmail() {
  const ctx = V('compose-subject')?.value || V('compose-to')?.value || 'professional business email';
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'email',context:ctx});
    if(V('compose-body')) V('compose-body').value = r.content;
    toast('Email written by AI!','success');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

async function sendGmailCompose() {
  const to = V('compose-to')?.value;
  const subject = V('compose-subject')?.value;
  const body = V('compose-body')?.value;
  if (!to || !body) { toast('To and Message are required','error'); return; }
  const email = [`To: ${to}`,`Subject: ${subject||'(No subject)'}`,`From: ${STATE.gmailEmail||'me'}`,
    'Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8','MIME-Version: 1.0','',body].join('\r\n');
  const encoded = btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(email))).replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_').replace(/=+$/,'');
  try {
    const r = await fetch('https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/send', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ raw: encoded })
    });
    if (!r.ok) { const e=await r.json(); throw new Error(e.error?.message||'Send failed'); }
    await api('/messages','POST',{channel:'email',subject,body,direction:'outbound'}).catch(()=>{});
    closeModal(); toast('Email sent via Gmail! ✅','success');
  } catch(e) { toast('Gmail error: '+e.message,'error'); }
}

async function sendViaBackend() {
  const to = V('compose-to')?.value;
  const subject = V('compose-subject')?.value;
  const body = V('compose-body')?.value;
  if (!to || !body) { toast('To and Message are required','error'); return; }
  try {
    await api('/email/send','POST',{ to, subject, body });
    closeModal(); toast('Email sent via server SMTP! ✅','success');
  } catch(e) { toast('Server SMTP error: '+e.message+'. Check Settings → Email SMTP.','error',6000); }
}

}

    content(`

      </div>
      <div style="max-width:540px;margin:0 auto">
        <div class="card" style="text-align:center;padding:48px">
          <div style="font-size:56px;margin-bottom:16px">📧</div>

          <div class="ai-insight" style="text-align:left;margin-bottom:20px">
            <div class="ai-insight-title">✅ What you can do:</div>
            <div class="ai-insight-text">• Read your Gmail inbox in real-time<br>• Send emails directly from NexusCRM<br>• AI-powered reply drafting<br>• Save important emails to CRM contacts<br>• AI sentiment analysis on incoming emails<br>• Bulk email campaigns via backend SMTP</div>

      </div>`);
    return;
  }
  // Connected — show inbox
  content(`
    <div class="page-header">
      <div><div class="page-title">📧 Gmail Inbox</div><div class="page-subtitle" id="gmail-status-sub">Loading...</div></div>
      <div class="header-actions">
        <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiAnalyzeInbox()">🤖 AI Analyze Inbox</button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="loadFullGmail()">🔄 Refresh</button>
        <button class="btn btn-gmail" onclick="openGmailCompose()">✏️ Compose</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:280px 1fr;gap:20px">
      <div>
        <div class="card card-sm" style="margin-bottom:12px">
          ${['📥 Inbox','⭐ Starred','📤 Sent','📝 Drafts','🗑️ Trash'].map(l=>`<div class="nav-item" style="margin:0;padding:8px;border-radius:6px" onclick="filterGmail('${l.split(' ')[1].toLowerCase()}')">${l}</div>`).join('')}
        </div>
        <div class="card card-sm">
          <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px;font-size:12px">AI Actions</div>
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" style="width:100%;margin-bottom:6px" onclick="aiSummarizeInbox()">📊 Summarize Inbox</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="width:100%;margin-bottom:6px" onclick="aiPrioritizeEmails()">🎯 Prioritize Emails</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="width:100%" onclick="aiDraftCampaign()">📢 Draft Campaign</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card" style="padding:0;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
          <input placeholder="🔍 Search emails..." style="flex:1;max-width:300px" oninput="searchGmailEmails(this.value)">
          <span id="gmail-count" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text3)"></span>
        </div>
        <div id="gmail-full-list" style="height:calc(100vh - 280px);overflow-y:auto;padding:8px"></div>
      </div>
    </div>`);
  loadFullGmail();
};

async function loadFullGmail() {
  const list = V('gmail-full-list');
  const sub = V('gmail-status-sub');
  if (list) list.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div>Loading Gmail...</div>';
  try {
    const r = await fetch('https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages?maxResults=30&q=in:inbox', {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}` }
    });
    if (!r.ok) { throw new Error('Gmail API error — token may be expired'); }
    const data = await r.json();
    const msgs = data.messages || [];
    const details = await Promise.all(
      msgs.slice(0,20).map(m =>
        fetch(`https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/${m.id}?format=metadata&metadataHeaders=From&metadataHeaders=Subject&metadataHeaders=Date`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}` }
        }).then(r=>r.json())
      )
    );
    STATE.gmailEmails = details;
    const unread = details.filter(m=>m.labelIds?.includes('UNREAD')).length;
    if (sub) sub.textContent = `${details.length} emails • ${unread} unread • ${STATE.gmailEmail}`;
    if (V('gmail-count')) V('gmail-count').textContent = `${details.length} messages`;
    updateGmailBadge(unread);
    renderFullGmail(details);
  } catch(e) {
    if (list) list.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">⚠️</div><div class="empty-title">${e.message}</div><button class="btn btn-gmail" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="connectGmail()">Reconnect Gmail</button></div>`;
  }
}

function renderFullGmail(emails) {
  const list = V('gmail-full-list');
  if (!list) return;
  list.innerHTML = emails.map(m => {
    const hdrs = {};
    (m.payload?.headers||[]).forEach(h=>hdrs[h.name]=h.value);
    const fromRaw = (hdrs.From||'Unknown').replace(/<[^>]+>/,'').trim();
    const from = esc(fromRaw);
    const subjRaw = hdrs.Subject||'(No subject)';
    const subj = esc(subjRaw);
    const date = hdrs.Date ? new Date(hdrs.Date).toLocaleDateString([],{month:'short',day:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}) : '';
    const unread = m.labelIds?.includes('UNREAD');
    return `
      <div class="gmail-email-row ${unread?'unread':''}" onclick="openEmail('${m.id}')">
        <div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${unread?'var(--accent)':'transparent'};flex-shrink:0;margin-top:3px"></div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px">
            <div class="gmail-from">${from}</div>
            <div class="gmail-time">${date}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="gmail-subject">${subj}</div>
          ${m.snippet?`<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;margin-top:2px">${esc(m.snippet)}</div>`:''}
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-shrink:0;margin-left:8px">
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="event.stopPropagation();aiReplyEmail('${escAttr(fromRaw)}','${escAttr(subjRaw)}','${m.id}')" style="padding:3px 8px;font-size:10px">🤖</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

function searchGmailEmails(q) {
  const filtered = STATE.gmailEmails.filter(m => {
    const hdrs = {};
    (m.payload?.headers||[]).forEach(h=>hdrs[h.name]=h.value);
    return (hdrs.From||'').toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ||
           (hdrs.Subject||'').toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ||
           (m.snippet||'').toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase());
  });
  renderFullGmail(filtered);
}
async function filterGmail(label) {
  const list = V('gmail-full-list');
  if (list) list.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
  const labelMap = { inbox:'in:inbox', starred:'is:starred', sent:'in:sent', drafts:'in:drafts', trash:'in:trash' };
  try {
    const r = await fetch(`https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages?maxResults=20&q=${labelMap[label]||'in:inbox'}`,{headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}`}});
    const data = await r.json();
    const msgs = data.messages||[];
    const details = await Promise.all(msgs.slice(0,15).map(m=>fetch(`https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/${m.id}?format=metadata&metadataHeaders=From&metadataHeaders=Subject&metadataHeaders=Date`,{headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${STATE.gmailToken}`}}).then(r=>r.json())));
    renderFullGmail(details);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiAnalyzeInbox() {
  toast('AI analyzing your inbox...','info',5000);
  const emails = STATE.gmailEmails.slice(0,10).map(m=>{
    const hdrs={}; (m.payload?.headers||[]).forEach(h=>hdrs[h.name]=h.value);
    return {from:hdrs.From,subject:hdrs.Subject,snippet:m.snippet,unread:m.labelIds?.includes('UNREAD')};
  });
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Analyze these ${emails.length} recent emails and give: 1) Priority emails requiring urgent response, 2) Potential sales opportunities, 3) Suggested actions. Emails: ${JSON.stringify(emails)}`});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🤖 AI Inbox Analysis</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`, 'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiSummarizeInbox() {
  toast('Summarizing...','info');
  const emails = STATE.gmailEmails.slice(0,10).map(m=>{
    const hdrs={}; (m.payload?.headers||[]).forEach(h=>hdrs[h.name]=h.value);
    return {from:hdrs.From,subject:hdrs.Subject};
  });
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Summarize my inbox in 3 bullet points. Focus on what needs my attention most urgently. My emails: ${JSON.stringify(emails)}`});
    toast('AI: ' + r.content.slice(0,150),'info',8000);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiPrioritizeEmails() {
  const emails = STATE.gmailEmails.slice(0,10).map(m=>{
    const hdrs={}; (m.payload?.headers||[]).forEach(h=>hdrs[h.name]=h.value);
    return {subject:hdrs.Subject,from:hdrs.From};
  });
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Rank these emails by urgency (1=most urgent). Return as JSON array: [{"subject":string,"priority":1-5,"reason":string}]. Emails: ${JSON.stringify(emails)}`});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🎯 Email Priority</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiDraftCampaign() {
  openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📢 AI Email Campaign</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>Campaign goal</label><input id="camp-goal" placeholder="e.g. Re-engage old leads with a special offer"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Target audience</label><input id="camp-audience" placeholder="e.g. leads who haven't replied in 30 days"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="doDraftCampaign()">✨ Draft Campaign</button></div>`);
}
async function doDraftCampaign() {
  const goal=V('camp-goal')?.value; const aud=V('camp-audience')?.value;
  if(!goal){toast('Enter campaign goal','error');return;}
  toast('Writing campaign...','info',6000);
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Write a 3-email drip campaign for: "${goal}" targeting: ${aud||'warm leads'}. Include Subject line, Email body, and Timing for each email. Make each email compelling and different.`,max_tokens:1500});
    closeModal();
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📧 Email Campaign</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2);max-height:500px;overflow-y:auto">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`, 'modal-xl');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

      const el=V('forecast-body'); if(!el) return;
      const bars = (f.buckets||[]).map(b=>`<div style="flex:1">
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">${esc(b.label)}</div>

      const el=V('dash-deals'); if(!el) return;

      el.innerHTML = r.length ? r.map(d=>`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">
        <div><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px">${esc(d.title)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${esc(d.contact_name||'No contact')}</div></div>
        <div style="text-align:right">${stageBadge(d.stage)}<div style="color:var(--green);font-weight:700;font-size:13px;margin-top:2px">${$$$(d.value)}</div></div>
      </div>`).join('') : '<div class="empty-state" style="padding:20px"><div class="empty-text">No deals yet</div></div>';
    }).catch(()=>{});
    api('/tasks').then(d=>{
      const el=V('dash-tasks'); if(!el) return;
      const r=(d.tasks||[]).filter(t=>t.status==='todo').slice(0,5);
      el.innerHTML = r.length ? r.map(t=>`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">
        <input type="checkbox" onchange="completeTask(${t.id},this)" style="width:16px;height:16px;cursor:pointer;accent-color:var(--accent)">
        <div style="flex:1"><div style="font-size:13px">${esc(t.title)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${esc(t.due_date||'No due date')}</div></div>
        ${priorityBadge(t.priority)}
      </div>`).join('') : '<div class="empty-state" style="padding:20px"><div class="empty-text">✅ All done!</div></div>';
    }).catch(()=>{});
  } catch(e) { content(`<div class="card"><p style="color:var(--red)">Error: ${e.message}. Is the server running?</p><button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="views.dashboard()">Retry</button></div>`); }
};
async function loadDailyBrief(force) {
  const el = V('daily-brief'); if (!el) return;
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/brief');
    const b = r.brief || '';
    el.innerHTML = `<div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(b)}</div>`;
  } catch { el.innerHTML = ''; }
}
async function completeTask(id, cb) {
  try { await api(`/tasks/${id}`,'PATCH',{status:cb.checked?'done':'todo'}); if(cb.checked) toast('Task done! ✅','success'); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); cb.checked=!cb.checked; }
}

// ── CONTACTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
views.contacts = async function(search='') {
  content('<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>');
  try {
    let q = search ? `?search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}` : '';
    if (tagFilter) q += (q ? '&' : '?') + 'tag=' + encodeURIComponent(tagFilter);
    const d = await api('/contacts' + q);
    const contacts = d.contacts || [];
    // For the tag chips we want every tag in the workspace — when a filter is
    // active the filtered list would hide them, so fetch the unfiltered set.
    let tagSource = contacts;
    if (tagFilter || search) {
      const raw = await api('/contacts' + (search ? `?search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}` : '')).catch(() => ({ contacts: [] }));
      tagSource = raw.contacts || [];
    }
    const allTags = [...new Set((tagSource||[]).flatMap(c => String(c.tags||'').split(',').map(t=>t.trim()).filter(Boolean)))];
    content(`
      <div class="page-header">
        <div><div class="page-title">Contacts</div><div class="page-subtitle">${$$(contacts.length)} contacts${d.total?` of ${$$(d.total)}`:''}</div></div>
        <div class="header-actions">
          <input placeholder="🔍 Search..." style="width:200px" value="${escAttr(search)}" oninput="debounce(q=>views.contacts(q),350)(this.value)">
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiScoreAllLeads()">🤖 AI Score All</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="importContactsCSV()">📂 Import CSV</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openAddContact()">+ Add Contact</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${allTags.length ? `<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">${allTags.map(t=>`<span class="badge ${tagFilter===t?'badge-purple':'badge-gray'}" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="toggleTagFilter('${escAttr(t)}')">${esc(t)}${tagFilter===t?' ✕':''}</span>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
      <div class="table-wrap">
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Company</th><th>Stage</th><th>Tags</th><th>AI Score</th><th>Phone</th><th>Last Updated</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          ${contacts.length ? contacts.map(c=>`<tr>
            <td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
              <div style="width:30px;height:30px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),var(--accent2));border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">${esc((c.name[0]||'?')).toUpperCase()}</div>
              <div><div style="font-weight:600">${esc(c.name)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${esc(c.email||'')}</div></div></div></td>
            <td>${esc(c.company||'—')}</td>
            <td>${stageBadge(c.stage)}</td>
            <td>${String(c.tags||'').split(',').map(t=>t.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0,3).map(t=>`<span class="badge badge-purple" style="font-size:9px;margin-right:3px">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')||'<span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:11px">—</span>'}</td>
            <td>${aiScoreBadge(c.ai_score)}</td>
            <td>${c.phone ? `<a href="${waLink(c.phone)}" target="_blank" style="color:var(--green);text-decoration:none" title="Open WhatsApp chat">💚 ${esc(c.phone)}</a>` : '—'}</td>
            <td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${timeAgo(c.updated_at)}</td>
            <td><div style="display:flex;gap:4px">
              <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="viewContact(${c.id})">View</button>
              <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="scoreContact(${c.id})">🤖</button>
              ${c.email?`<button class="btn btn-gmail btn-sm" onclick="quickEmailContact('${escAttr(c.email)}','${escAttr(c.name)}')">📧</button>`:''}
              <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteContact(${c.id})">✕</button>
            </div></td>
          </tr>`).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="8"><div class="empty-state">No contacts found</div></td></tr>'}
        </tbody>
      </table></div>`);
  } catch(e) { content(`<div class="card"><p style="color:var(--red)">Error: ${e.message}</p></div>`); }
};
function quickEmailContact(email, name) {
  if (STATE.gmailToken) {
    openGmailCompose();
    setTimeout(() => { if(V('compose-to')) V('compose-to').value = email; }, 100);
  } else {
    window.open(`mailto:${email}?subject=Hello ${name}`);
  }
}
function cfObject(cf) {
  if (!cf) return {};
  if (typeof cf === 'object') return cf;
  try { const p = JSON.parse(cf); return p && typeof p === 'object' ? p : {}; } catch { return {}; }
}
function addCustomFieldRow() {
  const r = document.createElement('div');
  r.style.cssText = 'display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 28px;gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px';
  r.innerHTML = '<input placeholder="Label" class="cf-label"><input placeholder="Value" class="cf-value"><button type="button" onclick="this.closest(\'div\').remove()" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--red);cursor:pointer;font-size:16px">×</button>';
  const box = V('c-custom-fields'); if (box) box.appendChild(r);
}
function collectCustomFields() {
  const out = {};
  document.querySelectorAll('#c-custom-fields > div').forEach(row => {
    const label = row.querySelector('.cf-label')?.value?.trim();
    const value = row.querySelector('.cf-value')?.value?.trim();
    if (label) out[label] = value || '';
  });
  return out;
}
function openAddContact() {
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">Add Contact</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group"><label>Full Name *</label><input id="c-name" placeholder="Jane Smith" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')addContact()"></div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Email</label><input id="c-email" type="email" placeholder="jane@company.com"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group"><label>Phone / WhatsApp</label><input id="c-phone" placeholder="+1 555 000 0000"></div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Company</label><input id="c-company" placeholder="Acme Inc"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group"><label>Stage</label><select id="c-stage"><option value="lead">Lead</option><option value="prospect">Prospect</option><option value="qualified">Qualified</option><option value="customer">Customer</option></select></div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Source</label><select id="c-source"><option>manual</option><option>website</option><option>referral</option><option>social</option><option>ad</option><option>email</option></select></div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Tags (comma separated)</label><input id="c-tags" placeholder="vip, hot-lead, newsletter"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Custom Fields</label>
        <div id="c-custom-fields"><div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 28px;gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px"><input placeholder="Label (e.g. Birthday)" class="cf-label"><input placeholder="Value" class="cf-value"><span></span></div></div>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" type="button" onclick="addCustomFieldRow()">+ Add Field</button>
      </div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Notes</label><textarea id="c-notes" placeholder="Any additional notes..."></textarea></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="addContact()">Add Contact</button>
    </div>`);
  setTimeout(()=>V('c-name')?.focus(),100);
}
async function addContact() {
  if (!V('c-name').value.trim()) { toast('Name is required','error'); return; }
  const phone = V('c-phone').value.trim();
  if (phone && !phoneLooksIntl(phone)) {
    toast('⚠️ Saved — but that phone number looks like it\'s missing a country code (e.g. +1, +44). The WhatsApp button may open the wrong chat until it\'s fixed.', 'error', 7000);
  }
  try {
    await api('/contacts','POST',{name:V('c-name').value,email:V('c-email').value,phone,company:V('c-company').value,stage:V('c-stage').value,source:V('c-source').value,notes:V('c-notes').value,tags:V('c-tags')?.value||'',custom_fields:collectCustomFields()});
    closeModal(); toast('Contact added! ✅','success'); views.contacts();
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function deleteContact(id) {
  if (!confirm('Delete this contact? This cannot be undone.')) return;
  try { await api(`/contacts/${id}`,'DELETE'); toast('Deleted','success'); views.contacts(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function scoreContact(id) {
  const t = toast('AI scoring lead...','info',8000);
  try {
    const d = await api(`/ai/score-lead/${id}`,'POST',{});
    t.remove?.();
    if (d.live === false) toast(`Score: ${d.score}/100 — ${d.reason} (local estimate — AI provider unreachable${d.error?': '+d.error:''})`,'warning',8000);
    else toast(`AI Score: ${d.score}/100 — ${d.reason}`,'success',6000);
    views.contacts();
  } catch(e) { t.remove?.(); toast('Scoring failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiScoreAllLeads() {
  toast('Scoring all unscored leads...','info',8000);
  try {
    const d = await api('/contacts');
    const unscored = (d.contacts||[]).filter(c=>!c.ai_score).slice(0,15);
    if (!unscored.length) { toast('All leads already scored!','info'); return; }
    let liveCount = 0, localCount = 0;
    for (const c of unscored) {
      const r = await api(`/ai/score-lead/${c.id}`,'POST',{}).catch(()=>null);
      if (r) { if (r.live === false) localCount++; else liveCount++; }
    }
    if (localCount && !liveCount) toast(`Scored ${unscored.length} leads using local estimates — your AI provider wasn't reachable. Check Settings → AI Providers.`,'warning',7000);
    else if (localCount) toast(`Scored ${unscored.length} leads (${liveCount} by AI, ${localCount} local estimate due to a connection hiccup). ✅`,'success',6000);
    else toast(`Scored ${unscored.length} leads with AI! ✅`,'success');
    views.contacts();
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

    openModal(`

          <div><div class="modal-title">${esc(c.name)}</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2)">${esc(c.email||'')} ${c.phone?'• '+esc(c.phone):''} ${c.company?'• '+esc(c.company):''}</div></div>

        ${String(c.tags||'').split(',').map(t=>t.trim()).filter(Boolean).length?`<div class="form-group"><label>Tags</label><div>${String(c.tags||'').split(',').map(t=>t.trim()).filter(Boolean).map(t=>`<span class="badge badge-purple" style="margin-right:4px">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div>`:''}
        ${Object.keys(cfObject(c.custom_fields)).length?`<div class="form-group"><label>Custom Fields</label><div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px">${Object.entries(cfObject(c.custom_fields)).map(([k,v])=>`<div style="background:var(--card2);padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px"><span style="color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">${esc(k)}:</span> ${esc(v)}</div>`).join('')}</div></div>`:''}
        ${c.notes?`<div class="form-group"><label>Notes</label><div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.6;background:var(--card2);padding:10px;border-radius:8px">${esc(c.notes)}</div></div>`:''}
        ${c.deals?.length?`<div style="font-weight:600;margin-bottom:8px;font-size:13px">Deals (${c.deals.length})</div>${c.deals.map(d=>`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">${esc(d.title)} ${stageBadge(d.stage)} <span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:700">${$$$(d.value)}</span></div>`).join('')}`:''}

function importContactsCSV() {
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📂 Import Contacts (CSV)</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="ai-insight" style="margin-bottom:14px">
        <div class="ai-insight-title">CSV Format</div>
        <div class="ai-insight-text">First row must be headers. Supported columns: name, email, phone, company, stage, notes, source</div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Paste CSV data</label><textarea id="csv-data" rows="8" placeholder="name,email,phone,company&#10;John Smith,john@example.com,+1555001,Acme Inc"></textarea></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="processCSVImport()">Import Contacts</button></div>`);
}
async function processCSVImport() {
  const raw = V('csv-data')?.value;
  if (!raw) { toast('Paste CSV data first','error'); return; }
  const lines = raw.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
  const headers = parseCSVLine(lines[0] || '').map(h=>h.trim().toLowerCase());
  let imported = 0, errors = 0;
  for (let i=1; i<lines.length; i++) {
    if (!lines[i].trim()) continue;
    const vals = parseCSVLine(lines[i]).map(v=>v.trim());
    const obj = {};
    headers.forEach((h,idx)=>obj[h]=vals[idx]||'');
    if (!obj.name) continue;
    try { await api('/contacts','POST',obj); imported++; }
    catch { errors++; }
  }
  closeModal();
  toast(`Imported ${imported} contacts${errors?`, ${errors} failed`:''}! ✅`,'success',5000);
  views.contacts();
}

// ── PIPELINE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
const PIPELINE_STAGES = ['lead','prospect','qualified','proposal','negotiation','won','lost'];
const STAGE_LABELS = {lead:'🌱 Lead',prospect:'👁️ Prospect',qualified:'✅ Qualified',proposal:'📋 Proposal',negotiation:'🤝 Negotiation',won:'🏆 Won',lost:'❌ Lost'};
let dragId = null;

views.pipeline = async function() {
  content('<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>');
  try {
    const d = await api('/deals');
    const deals = d.deals||[];
    const activeDeals = deals.filter(x=>!['won','lost'].includes(x.stage));
    const total = activeDeals.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.value,0);
    const won = deals.filter(x=>x.stage==='won').reduce((a,d)=>a+d.value,0);
    content(`
      <div class="page-header">
        <div>
          <div class="page-title">Pipeline</div>
          <div class="page-subtitle">Active: ${$$$(total)} • Won: ${$$$(won)} • ${$$(activeDeals.length)} open deals</div>
        </div>
        <div class="header-actions">
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiAnalyzePipeline()">🤖 AI Analyze</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="showDealRisks()">🚨 AI Risks</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openAddDeal()">+ Add Deal</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="kanban">
        ${PIPELINE_STAGES.map(stage => {
          const stageDeals = deals.filter(d=>d.stage===stage);
          const stageVal = stageDeals.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.value,0);
          return `<div class="kanban-col" data-stage="${stage}" ondragover="event.preventDefault()" ondrop="dropDeal(event,'${stage}')">
            <div class="kanban-col-header">
              <div class="kanban-col-title">${STAGE_LABELS[stage]}</div>
              <div class="kanban-col-count">${stageDeals.length}</div>
            </div>
            ${stageVal>0?`<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--green);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px">${$$$(stageVal)}</div>`:''}
            ${stageDeals.map(deal=>`
              <div class="deal-card" draggable="true" data-id="${deal.id}" ondragstart="dragDeal(event,${deal.id})" onclick="openDealDetail(${deal.id})">
                <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:3px">${esc(deal.title)}</div>
                <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--green);font-weight:700">${$$$(deal.value)}</div>
                <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:5px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between">
                  <span>👤 ${esc(deal.contact_name||'No contact')}</span>
                  <span>${deal.probability||0}%</span>
                </div>
                ${deal.close_date?`<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:3px">📅 ${deal.close_date}</div>`:''}
              </div>`).join('')}
            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="width:100%;margin-top:4px;opacity:.6" onclick="openAddDeal(null,null,'${stage}')">+ Add</button>
          </div>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>`);
  } catch(e) { content(`<div class="card"><p style="color:var(--red)">Error: ${e.message}</p></div>`); }
};
async function showDealRisks() {
  toast('Scanning for risky deals...','info',5000);
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/deal-risks');
    const risks = r.risks || [];
    if (!risks.length) { toast('No risky deals found 🎉','success'); return; }
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🚨 Deal Risks</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        ${risks.map(x=>`<div class="card card-sm" style="margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
          <span class="badge ${x.risk==='stale'?'badge-red':'badge-yellow'}">${x.risk==='stale'?`${x.age_days}d stale`:'low %'}</span>
          <div style="flex:1"><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px">${esc(x.title)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${esc(x.note)}</div></div>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="closeModal();openDealDetail(${x.id})">Open</button>
        </div>`).join('')}
      </div>`, 'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
function dragDeal(e,id) { dragId=id; e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move'; }
async function dropDeal(e,stage) {
  e.preventDefault(); if(!dragId) return;
  try { await api(`/deals/${dragId}`,'PATCH',{stage}); views.pipeline(); }
  catch(er) { toast(er.message,'error'); } dragId=null;
}
function openAddDeal(contactId=null,contactName=null,defaultStage='lead') {
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">Add Deal</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>Deal Title *</label><input id="d-title" placeholder="e.g. Website Redesign for Acme"></div>
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group"><label>Value ($)</label><input id="d-value" type="number" value="0" min="0"></div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Stage</label><select id="d-stage">${PIPELINE_STAGES.map(s=>`<option value="${s}"${s===defaultStage?' selected':''}>${STAGE_LABELS[s]}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group"><label>Win Probability %</label><input id="d-prob" type="number" value="20" min="0" max="100"></div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Close Date</label><input id="d-close" type="date"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Notes</label><textarea id="d-notes" placeholder="Deal notes..."></textarea></div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="addDeal(${contactId||'null'})">Add Deal</button></div>`);
}
async function addDeal(contactId) {
  if (!V('d-title').value.trim()) { toast('Title required','error'); return; }
  try {
    await api('/deals','POST',{title:V('d-title').value,contact_id:contactId||null,value:parseFloat(V('d-value').value)||0,stage:V('d-stage').value,probability:parseInt(V('d-prob').value)||20,close_date:V('d-close').value,notes:V('d-notes').value});
    closeModal(); toast('Deal added! ✅','success'); views.pipeline();
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function openDealDetail(id) {
  try {
    const d2 = await api('/deals');
    const deal = (d2.deals||[]).find(x=>x.id===id); if(!deal) return;
    openModal(`
      <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">${deal.title}</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <div class="grid grid-3" style="margin-bottom:14px">
          <div class="card card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Value</div><div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:var(--green)">${$$$(deal.value)}</div></div>
          <div class="card card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Stage</div>${stageBadge(deal.stage)}</div>
          <div class="card card-sm"><div class="stat-label">Win %</div><div style="font-weight:700">${deal.probability}%</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Stage</label><select id="edit-stage">${PIPELINE_STAGES.map(s=>`<option value="${s}"${s===deal.stage?' selected':''}>${STAGE_LABELS[s]}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
        <div class="form-row">
          <div class="form-group"><label>Value</label><input id="edit-val" type="number" value="${deal.value}"></div>
          <div class="form-group"><label>Probability %</label><input id="edit-prob" type="number" value="${deal.probability}"></div>
        </div>
        ${deal.notes?`<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text2);padding:10px;background:var(--card2);border-radius:8px">${deal.notes}</div>`:''}
      </div>
      <div class="modal-footer">
        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="deleteDeal(${deal.id})">Delete</button>
        <button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="aiDealUpdate(${deal.id})">📧 AI Update Email</button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="updateDeal(${deal.id})">Save Changes</button>
      </div>`);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiDealUpdate(id) {
  try {
    const d2 = await api('/deals');
    const deal = (d2.deals||[]).find(x=>x.id===id); if(!deal) return;
    toast('Writing a status update email...','info');
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'followup_email', context:`Status update on deal "${deal.title}" — currently at stage ${deal.stage}, value ${deal.value||0}, probability ${deal.probability||0}%.`, target: deal.contact_name || undefined});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📧 Deal Status Email</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body"><textarea id="deal-email-body" style="width:100%;min-height:160px">${esc(r.content)}</textarea></div>
      <div class="modal-footer" style="gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="saveSnippet(V('deal-email-body').value)">📌 Save</button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText(V('deal-email-body').value);toast('Copied!','success')">📋 Copy</button>
        <button class="btn btn-gmail" onclick="saveDealEmailToCRM()">💾 Save to CRM</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="closeModal()">Done</button>
      </div>`, 'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function saveDealEmailToCRM() {
  const body = V('deal-email-body')?.value;
  if (!body) { toast('Nothing to save','error'); return; }
  try {
    await api('/messages','POST',{channel:'email',subject:'Deal update',body,direction:'outbound',ai_generated:1});
    toast('Saved to CRM Messages ✅','success'); closeModal();
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function updateDeal(id) {
  try { await api(`/deals/${id}`,'PATCH',{stage:V('edit-stage').value,value:parseFloat(V('edit-val').value),probability:parseInt(V('edit-prob').value)}); closeModal(); toast('Updated!','success'); views.pipeline(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function deleteDeal(id) {
  if(!confirm('Delete this deal?')) return;
  try { await api(`/deals/${id}`,'DELETE'); closeModal(); toast('Deleted','success'); views.pipeline(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiAnalyzePipeline() {
  toast('Analyzing pipeline with AI...','info',5000);
  try {
    const d = await api('/deals');
    const r = await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Analyze this sales pipeline and give 3 specific, actionable recommendations. Data: ${JSON.stringify((d.deals||[]).map(x=>({title:x.title,stage:x.stage,value:x.value,probability:x.probability,contact:x.contact_name})))}. Focus on increasing close rates and revenue.`});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🤖 AI Pipeline Analysis</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`, 'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast('AI failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}

// ── TASKS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
views.tasks = async function() {
  content('<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>');
  try {
    const d = await api('/tasks');
    const tasks = d.tasks||[];
    const todo = tasks.filter(t=>t.status==='todo');
    content(`
      <div class="page-header">
        <div><div class="page-title">Tasks</div><div class="page-subtitle">${todo.length} pending, ${tasks.length-todo.length} done</div></div>
        <div class="header-actions">
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiPrioritizeTasks()">🤖 Prioritize</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openAddTask()">+ Add Task</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="tabs" style="margin-bottom:16px">
        <div class="tab active" onclick="filterTasks('all',this)">All (${tasks.length})</div>
        <div class="tab" onclick="filterTasks('todo',this)">Todo (${todo.length})</div>
        <div class="tab" onclick="filterTasks('done',this)">Done (${tasks.length-todo.length})</div>
      </div>
      <div class="table-wrap"><table id="tasks-table">
        <thead><tr><th style="width:30px"></th><th>Task</th><th>Contact</th><th>Priority</th><th>AI Rank</th><th>Due Date</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>${tasks.length ? tasks.map(t=>`<tr data-status="${t.status}" data-id="${t.id}" style="${t.status==='done'?'opacity:.55':''}">  
          <td><input type="checkbox" ${t.status==='done'?'checked':''} onchange="toggleTask(${t.id},this)" style="width:16px;height:16px;cursor:pointer;accent-color:var(--accent)"></td>
          <td><div style="font-weight:${t.status!=='done'?'600':'400'}">${esc(t.title)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${esc(t.description||'')}</div></td>
          <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2)">${esc(t.contact_name||'—')}</td>
          <td>${priorityBadge(t.priority)}</td>
          <td class="ai-rank-cell" style="font-size:12px">—</td>
          <td style="font-size:12px">${t.due_date||'—'}</td>
          <td><div style="display:flex;gap:4px"><button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" title="AI: write the follow-up email for this task" onclick="aiTaskEmail(${t.id},'${escAttr(t.title)}','${escAttr(t.description||'')}','${escAttr(t.contact_name||'')}')">🤖</button><button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteTask(${t.id})">✕</button></div></td>
        </tr>`).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="6"><div class="empty-state">No tasks yet</div></td></tr>'}</tbody>
      </table></div>`);
    api('/ai/score-tasks').then(r=>{
      const map = {};
      (r.tasks||[]).forEach(x => { if (x && x.id) map[x.id] = x; });
      window.__taskScores = map;
      document.querySelectorAll('#tasks-table tbody tr[data-id]').forEach(row => {
        const cell = row.querySelector('.ai-rank-cell');
        const sc = map[parseInt(row.getAttribute('data-id'))];
        if (cell && sc) cell.innerHTML = `<span class="badge ${sc.score>=70?'badge-red':sc.score>=40?'badge-yellow':'badge-gray'}" title="${esc(sc.reason||'')}">${sc.score}</span>`;
      });
    }).catch(()=>{});
  } catch(e) { content(`<div class="card"><p>Error: ${e.message}</p></div>`); }
};
function filterTasks(status,tabEl) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active')); tabEl.classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('#tasks-table tbody tr').forEach(r=>r.style.display=(status==='all'||r.dataset.status===status)?'':'none');
}
function openAddTask() {
  openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">Add Task</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>Task Title *</label><input id="t-title" placeholder="What needs to be done?"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Description</label><textarea id="t-desc" placeholder="Optional details"></textarea></div>
      <div class="form-row">
        <div class="form-group"><label>Priority</label><select id="t-priority"><option value="urgent">🔴 Urgent</option><option value="high">🟠 High</option><option value="medium" selected>🟡 Medium</option><option value="low">🟢 Low</option></select></div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Due Date</label><input id="t-due" type="date"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="addTask()">Add Task</button></div>`);
}
async function addTask() {
  if(!V('t-title').value.trim()){toast('Title required','error');return;}
  try { await api('/tasks','POST',{title:V('t-title').value,description:V('t-desc').value,priority:V('t-priority').value,due_date:V('t-due').value}); closeModal(); toast('Task added!','success'); views.tasks(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function toggleTask(id,cb) {
  try { await api(`/tasks/${id}`,'PATCH',{status:cb.checked?'done':'todo'}); if(cb.checked) toast('Task done! ✅','success'); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); cb.checked=!cb.checked; }
}
async function deleteTask(id) {
  if(!confirm('Delete task?')) return;
  try { await api(`/tasks/${id}`,'DELETE'); toast('Deleted','success'); views.tasks(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
// ── C10: Contact AI Summary + Tag Suggestions ──
async function contactAISummary(id, name) {
  toast(`AI analyzing ${name}'s history...`,'info',8000);
  try {
    const r = await api(`/ai/contact-summary/${id}`);
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📋 Relationship Summary — ${esc(name)}</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body"><div class="ai-insight"><div class="ai-insight-text" style="white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.8">${esc(r.summary)}</div></div></div>`,'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast('Summary failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}
async function contactAITags(id, name) {
  toast('Suggesting tags...','info',6000);
  try {
    const r = await api(`/ai/tag-suggest/${id}`);
    const tags = r.tags || [];
    if (!tags.length) { toast('No tag suggestions — try again.','info'); return; }
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🏷️ Suggested Tags — ${esc(name)}</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px">${tags.map(t=>`<span class="badge badge-purple" style="padding:6px 12px;font-size:12px">${esc(t)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="applySuggestedTags(${id},'${escAttr(tags.join(','))}')">✅ Apply These Tags</button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      </div>`);
  } catch(e) { toast('Tag suggest failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}
async function applySuggestedTags(id, tags) {
  try {
    await api(`/contacts/${id}`,'PATCH',{ tags });
    closeModal(); toast('Tags applied ✅','success');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiTaskEmail(id, title, desc, contactName) {
  toast('Writing your follow-up email...','info');
  try {
    const ctx = `Follow up on the task: "${title}". ${desc ? 'Details: ' + desc : ''}`;
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'followup_email', context:ctx, target: contactName || undefined});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📧 Follow-up Email</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body"><textarea id="task-email-body" style="width:100%;min-height:180px">${esc(r.content)}</textarea></div>
      <div class="modal-footer" style="gap:8px">
        <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="saveSnippet(V('task-email-body').value)">📌 Save</button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText(V('task-email-body').value);toast('Copied!','success')">📋 Copy</button>
        <button class="btn btn-gmail" onclick="saveTaskEmailToCRM(${id})">💾 Save to CRM</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="closeModal()">Done</button>
      </div>`, 'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function saveTaskEmailToCRM(taskId) {
  const body = V('task-email-body')?.value;
  if (!body) { toast('Nothing to save','error'); return; }
  try {
    await api('/messages','POST',{channel:'email',subject:'Follow-up',body,direction:'outbound',ai_generated:1});
    toast('Saved to CRM Messages ✅','success'); closeModal();
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiPrioritizeTasks() {
  try {
    const d = await api('/tasks');
    const r = await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Review these tasks and tell me which 3 to focus on today and WHY. Tasks: ${JSON.stringify((d.tasks||[]).filter(t=>t.status==='todo').map(t=>({title:t.title,priority:t.priority,due:t.due_date})))}. Be specific and brief.`});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🤖 Today's Focus</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`);
  } catch(e) { toast('AI failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}

// ── INBOX (CRM Messages) ──────────────────────────────────────
views.inbox = async function() {
  content('<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>');
  try {
    const d = await api('/messages');
    const msgs = d.messages||[];
    content(`
      <div class="page-header">
        <div><div class="page-title">Messages</div><div class="page-subtitle">${msgs.length} CRM messages</div></div>
        <div class="header-actions">
          ${STATE.gmailToken ? `<button class="btn btn-gmail btn-sm" onclick="navigate('gmail')">📧 Gmail Inbox</button>` : `<button class="btn btn-gmail btn-sm" onclick="connectGmail()">Connect Gmail</button>`}
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiDraftMessage()">🤖 AI Compose</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openComposeMessage()">+ Compose</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="table-wrap"><table>
        <thead><tr><th>Contact</th><th>Channel</th><th>Subject</th><th>Preview</th><th>AI</th><th>Date</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>${msgs.length ? msgs.map(m=>`<tr style="cursor:pointer" onclick="viewCRMMessage(${m.id})">
          <td style="font-weight:600">${esc(m.contact_name||'—')}</td>
          <td><span class="badge ${m.channel==='whatsapp'?'badge-green':m.channel==='sms'?'badge-yellow':'badge-blue'}">${esc(m.channel)}</span></td>
          <td>${esc(m.subject||'—')}${m.direction==='inbound'?` <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" style="padding:2px 8px;font-size:10px" title="AI Smart Reply" onclick="event.stopPropagation();smartReplyFor('${escAttr((m.body||'').slice(0,300))}')">🤖</button>`:''}</td>
          <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);max-width:200px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${esc((m.body||'').slice(0,80))}</td>
          <td>${m.ai_generated?'<span class="badge badge-purple">🤖</span>':''}</td>
          <td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${timeAgo(m.created_at)}</td>
        </tr>`).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="6"><div class="empty-state">No messages yet</div></td></tr>'}</tbody>
      </table></div>`);
  } catch(e) { content(`<div class="card"><p>Error: ${e.message}</p></div>`); }
};
function openComposeMessage() {
  openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">Compose Message</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>Channel</label><select id="msg-channel"><option>email</option><option>whatsapp</option><option>sms</option></select></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Subject</label><input id="msg-subj" placeholder="Subject line"></div>
      <div class="form-group"><label>Message *</label><textarea id="msg-body" rows="5" placeholder="Type your message..."></textarea></div>
      <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiComposeInbox()" style="margin-bottom:8px">🤖 AI Generate</button>
    </div>
    <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="sendMsg()">Save to CRM</button></div>`);
}
async function aiComposeInbox() {
  try {
    const ch = V('msg-channel').value;
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:ch==='whatsapp'?'whatsapp':'email',context:V('msg-subj').value||'business follow-up'});
    if(V('msg-body')) V('msg-body').value = r.content;
    toast('AI generated!','success');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function sendMsg() {
  if(!V('msg-body').value.trim()){toast('Message required','error');return;}
  try { await api('/messages','POST',{channel:V('msg-channel').value,subject:V('msg-subj').value,body:V('msg-body').value}); closeModal(); toast('Saved!','success'); views.inbox(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiDraftMessage() {
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'email',context:'professional follow-up'});
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🤖 AI Drafted Message</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <textarea id="ai-draft-body" style="width:100%;min-height:200px">${esc(r.content)}</textarea>
      </div>
      <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Discard</button><button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="saveDraft()">Save to CRM</button></div>`);
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function saveDraft() {
  try { await api('/messages','POST',{body:V('ai-draft-body').value,channel:'email',ai_generated:1}); closeModal(); toast('Saved!','success'); views.inbox(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function smartReplyFor(text) {
  toast('Drafting reply options...','info',6000);
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/smart-reply','POST',{ text });
    const opts = r.options || [];
    window.__smartReplies = opts;
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🤖 Smart Replies</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">
          ${opts.map((o,i)=>`<div class="card card-sm" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="useSmartReply(${i})"><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:4px">Option ${i+1} — click to use</div><div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text2);white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(o)}</div></div>`).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>`,'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast('Smart reply failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}
function useSmartReply(i) {
  const t = (window.__smartReplies||[])[i];
  if (!t) { closeModal(); return; }
  if (STATE.gmailToken) { openGmailCompose(); setTimeout(()=>{ if(V('compose-body')) V('compose-body').value = t; },150); }
  else { navigator.clipboard?.writeText(t).then(()=>toast('Reply copied — paste it into your email! 📋','success')); }
  closeModal();
}
function viewCRMMessage(id) {
  // placeholder
  toast('Click email in Gmail for full view','info');
}
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// WHATSAPP OUTREACH
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
views.whatsapp = async function() {
  const d = await api('/contacts').catch(()=>({contacts:[]}));
  const contacts = d.contacts||[];
  content(`
    <div class="page-header">
      <div><div class="page-title">💚 WhatsApp Outreach</div><div class="page-subtitle">Message contacts directly via WhatsApp Web — free, no API needed</div></div>
      <div class="header-actions">
        <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="showBulkWA()">📢 Bulk Campaign</button>
        <button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="aiGenerateWAMessage()">🤖 AI Generate Message</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:300px 1fr;gap:20px">
      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:10px">Select Contact</div>
        <input placeholder="🔍 Search..." oninput="filterWAContacts(this.value)" style="margin-bottom:10px">
        <div id="wa-contact-list" style="max-height:500px;overflow-y:auto">
          ${contacts.length ? contacts.map(c=>`
            <div class="nav-item" style="margin:0 0 2px;padding:10px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px" onclick="selectWAContact(${c.id},'${escAttr(c.name)}','${waPhone(c.phone)}')"> 
              <div style="width:28px;height:28px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),var(--accent2));border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">${esc((c.name[0]||'?')).toUpperCase()}</div>
              <div><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600">${esc(c.name)}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${esc(c.phone||'No phone')}</div></div>
            </div>`).join('') : '<div class="empty-state" style="padding:20px"><div class="empty-text">No contacts yet</div></div>'}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card"><div id="wa-compose">
        <div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">💚</div><div class="empty-title">Select a contact</div><div class="empty-text">Choose a contact on the left to send them a WhatsApp message</div></div>
      </div></div>
    </div>`);
};
function filterWAContacts(q) { document.querySelectorAll('#wa-contact-list .nav-item').forEach(el=>el.style.display=el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())?'':'none'); }
function selectWAContact(id, name, phone) {
  V('wa-compose').innerHTML = `
    <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:15px;margin-bottom:16px">💚 Message to ${name}</div>
    ${!phone?'<div class="badge badge-red" style="margin-bottom:12px">⚠️ No phone number on file — add it in Contacts first</div>':''}
    <div class="form-group"><label>Quick Template</label>
      <select onchange="applyWATemplate(this.value,'${name}')" style="margin-bottom:8px">
