      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px">✅ Tasks by Status</div>
        <canvas id="chart-tasks" height="180" style="width:100%"></canvas>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px">
          <span class="badge badge-yellow">todo · ${taskList.filter(t=>t.status==='todo').length}</span>
          <span class="badge badge-green">done · ${taskList.filter(t=>t.status==='done').length}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid grid-2">
      <div class="card"><div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px">🤖 AI Usage</div>
        ${[['Total AI Calls',$$(usage.total||0),'var(--ai2)'],['Today',$$(usage.today||0),'var(--text)'],['Tokens Today',$$(usage.tokens_today||0),'var(--text)'],['Conversations',$$(usage.conversations||0),'var(--text)'],['CRM Messages',$$(usage.messages||0),'var(--text)']].map(([l,v,c])=>`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)"><span style="color:var(--text2);font-size:13px">${l}</span><span style="font-weight:700;color:${c}">${v}</span></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="card"><div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px">📉 Business</div>
        ${[['Pending Tasks',$$(stats.pending_tasks||0),'var(--text)'],['Upcoming Appts',$$(stats.upcoming_appointments||0),'var(--text)'],['Invoices Paid',$$$(stats.revenue_collected||0),'var(--green)'],['Sub-Accounts',$$(stats.sub_accounts||0),'var(--text)'],['Forms',$$(stats.forms||0),'var(--text)']].map(([l,v,c])=>`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)"><span style="color:var(--text2);font-size:13px">${l}</span><span style="font-weight:700;color:${c}">${v}</span></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>`);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    drawBarChart('chart-pipeline', stageCounts.filter(x=>x.count).map(x=>x.st), stageCounts.filter(x=>x.count).map(x=>x.count), '#6366f1');
    drawBarChart('chart-tasks', ['todo','done'], [taskList.filter(t=>t.status==='todo').length, taskList.filter(t=>t.status==='done').length], '#10b981');
  }, 50);
};
function drawBarChart(canvasId, labels, values, color) {
  const cv = V(canvasId); if (!cv) return;
  const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
  const w = cv.width = cv.clientWidth || 600;
  const h = cv.height = 200;
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
  const max = Math.max(1, ...values);
  const pad = 36, bw = (w - pad*2) / Math.max(1,labels.length) * 0.55;
  ctx.font = '11px Inter, sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  values.forEach((v,i)=>{
    const bh = Math.max(4, (v/max) * (h - 70));
    const x = pad + i * ((w-pad*2)/labels.length) + ((w-pad*2)/labels.length - bw)/2;
    const y = h - 40 - bh;
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y, 0, h-40);
    g.addColorStop(0, color); g.addColorStop(1, color + '55');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    const r = 5;
    ctx.roundRect ? ctx.roundRect(x, y, bw, bh, r) : ctx.rect(x, y, bw, bh);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e2e8f0'; ctx.fillText(String(v), x + bw/2, y - 6);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8'; ctx.fillText(String(labels[i]||''), x + bw/2, h - 24);
  });
}
async function exportReportsCSV() {
  const [contacts, deals, tasks, invoices] = await Promise.allSettled([api('/contacts?limit=2000'), api('/deals'), api('/tasks'), api('/invoices')]);
  const rows = [['Type','Name','Detail','Stage/Status','Value','Date']];
  (contacts.value?.contacts||[]).forEach(c=>rows.push(['Contact', c.name, c.email||c.phone||c.company||'', c.stage||'', '', c.created_at||'']));
  (deals.value?.deals||[]).forEach(d=>rows.push(['Deal', d.title, d.contact_name||'', d.stage||'', d.value||0, d.created_at||'']));
  (tasks.value?.tasks||[]).forEach(t=>rows.push(['Task', t.title, t.description||'', t.status||'', '', t.due_date||'']));
  (invoices.value?.invoices||[]).forEach(i=>rows.push(['Invoice', i.number, i.contact_name||'', i.status||'', i.total||0, i.created_at||'']));
  downloadCSV('nexuscrm-report-'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.csv', rows);
  toast('Report exported ✅','success');
}
async function aiExecutiveReport() { toast('Generating report...','info',5000); try{const [s,u]=await Promise.all([api('/stats'),api('/ai/usage')]); const r=await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Write an executive summary report for a business with these CRM metrics: ${JSON.stringify({...s,ai_usage:u})}. Include overall health, top 3 wins, 3 concerns, priority recommendations for next 30 days. Format professionally.`}); openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📈 Executive Report</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`, 'modal-lg');} catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');} }

// AI BOTS
const BOT_TYPES=[{id:'sales',icon:'💼',name:'Sales Bot',prompt:'You are a friendly sales qualification bot. Ask about their needs, budget, and timeline.',desc:'Qualifies leads'},{id:'support',icon:'🛡️',name:'Support Bot',prompt:'You are a helpful customer support bot. Be friendly and solve problems quickly.',desc:'Handles support'},{id:'booking',icon:'📅',name:'Booking Bot',prompt:'You are an appointment booking assistant. Help users schedule appointments.',desc:'Books appointments'},{id:'faq',icon:'❓',name:'FAQ Bot',prompt:'You are a knowledgeable FAQ bot. Answer questions helpfully and concisely.',desc:'Answers questions'},{id:'review',icon:'⭐',name:'Review Bot',prompt:'You are a review collector. Politely ask satisfied customers to leave reviews.',desc:'Collects reviews'}];
const BOT_STATE={messages:[],activeType:null};
views['ai-bots'] = function() {
  content(`<div class="page-header"><div><div class="page-title">🤖 AI Bots</div><div class="page-subtitle">Deploy AI chatbots for sales, support, and booking</div></div></div>
    <div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:260px 1fr;gap:20px">
      <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        ${BOT_TYPES.map(b=>`<div class="nav-item" style="border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;margin:0;padding:12px;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start" onclick="activateBot('${b.id}')">
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:4px"><span style="font-size:20px">${b.icon}</span><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px">${b.name}</div></div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">${b.desc}</div>
        </div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="card" style="padding:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:580px" id="bot-area">
        <div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">🤖</div><div class="empty-title">Select a Bot</div><div class="empty-text">Choose a bot to test it live</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>`);
};
function activateBot(id) {
  BOT_STATE.activeType=id; BOT_STATE.messages=[];
  const bot=BOT_TYPES.find(b=>b.id===id); if(!bot) return;
  const area=V('bot-area'); if(!area) return;
  area.innerHTML=`
    <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--ai),var(--ai2));padding:12px 16px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-shrink:0;border-radius:12px 12px 0 0">
      <span style="font-size:22px">${bot.icon}</span>
      <div><div style="color:#fff;font-weight:700">${bot.name}</div><div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.7);font-size:11px">${bot.desc}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="chat-messages" id="bot-messages" style="flex:1"></div>
    <div style="padding:12px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;gap:8px;flex-shrink:0">
      <div class="chat-input-wrap" style="flex:1"><textarea class="chat-input" id="bot-input" placeholder="Test this bot..." rows="1" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.shiftKey){event.preventDefault();sendBotMsg();}" oninput="autoResize(this)"></textarea></div>
      <button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="sendBotMsg()">&#10148;</button>
    </div>`;
  area.style.display='flex'; area.style.flexDirection='column';
  const msgs=V('bot-messages');
  const intro=document.createElement('div'); intro.className='chat-msg assistant';
  intro.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">${bot.icon}</div><div><div class="msg-bubble">Hi! I'm the ${bot.name}. ${bot.desc}. How can I help you today?</div></div>`;
  msgs?.appendChild(intro); if(msgs) msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
  BOT_STATE.messages=[{role:'system',content:bot.prompt}];
}
async function sendBotMsg() {
  const input=V('bot-input'); const text=input.value.trim(); if(!text) return;
  input.value=''; input.style.height='';
  const bot=BOT_TYPES.find(b=>b.id===BOT_STATE.activeType);
  BOT_STATE.messages.push({role:'user',content:text});
  const msgs=V('bot-messages'); if(!msgs) return;
  const userDiv=document.createElement('div'); userDiv.className='chat-msg user';
  userDiv.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar user-av">👤</div><div><div class="msg-bubble">${esc(text)}</div></div>`;
  msgs.appendChild(userDiv);
  const typing=document.createElement('div'); typing.className='chat-msg assistant';
  typing.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">${bot?.icon||'🤖'}</div><div class="chat-typing"><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div></div>`;
  msgs.appendChild(typing); msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
  try {
    const res=await chatStreamFetch(BOT_STATE.messages.slice(-8), bot?.prompt||'');
    typing.remove();
    if(!res.ok){let m='AI request failed';try{m=(await res.json()).error||m;}catch{}const eDiv=document.createElement('div');eDiv.className='chat-msg assistant';eDiv.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">${bot?.icon||'🤖'}</div><div><div class="msg-bubble">⚠️ ${esc(m)}</div></div>`;msgs.appendChild(eDiv);return;}
    const aiDiv=document.createElement('div'); aiDiv.className='chat-msg assistant';
    aiDiv.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">${bot?.icon||'🤖'}</div><div><div class="msg-bubble streaming-text"></div></div>`;
    msgs.appendChild(aiDiv); msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
    const bubble=aiDiv.querySelector('.msg-bubble');
    const reader=res.body.getReader(); const decoder=new TextDecoder(); let full=''; let failed=false;
    while(true){const{done,value}=await reader.read();if(done)break;for(const line of decoder.decode(value).split('\n')){if(!line.startsWith('data: '))continue;try{const d=JSON.parse(line.slice(6));if(d.delta&&bubble){full+=d.delta;bubble.innerHTML=esc(full).replace(/\n/g,'<br>');msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;}if(d.done&&bubble){bubble.classList.remove('streaming-text');}if(d.error&&bubble){failed=true;bubble.textContent='⚠️ '+d.error;bubble.classList.remove('streaming-text');}}catch{}}}
    if(!failed){const last=BOT_STATE.messages[BOT_STATE.messages.length-1];if(last&&last.role==='assistant'&&last.content==='')BOT_STATE.messages[BOT_STATE.messages.length-1]={role:'assistant',content:full};else BOT_STATE.messages.push({role:'assistant',content:full});}
  } catch { typing.remove(); }
}

  content(`<div class="page-header"><div><div class="page-title">✍️ Content AI Studio</div><div class="page-subtitle">Generate any content type with AI in seconds</div></div></div>

          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:8px">
            <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="generateContent()">🔄 Regenerate</button>
            <button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText(V('content-output-text').value);toast('Copied!','success')">📋 Copy</button>

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`);
};
function selectContentType(type,name) {
  currentContentType=type;
  V('content-ai-form').innerHTML=`
    <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:15px;margin-bottom:14px">${name}</div>
    <div class="form-group"><label>Context / Topic *</label><textarea id="ct-context" rows="3" placeholder="What should this ${name.toLowerCase()} be about?"></textarea></div>
    <div class="form-row"><div class="form-group"><label>Tone</label><select id="ct-tone"><option>professional</option><option>friendly</option><option>casual</option><option>formal</option><option>urgent</option></select></div><div class="form-group"><label>Target Audience</label><input id="ct-target" placeholder="Who is this for?"></div></div>
    <button class="btn btn-ai btn-lg" style="width:100%" onclick="generateContent()">✨ Generate ${name} with AI</button>`;
  V('content-ai-output').style.display='none';
}
async function generateContent() {
  if(!currentContentType){toast('Select a content type','error');return;}
  const ctx=V('ct-context')?.value; if(!ctx){toast('Add context','error');return;}
  toast('Generating...','info',4000);
  try {
    const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:currentContentType,tone:V('ct-tone')?.value,context:ctx,target:V('ct-target')?.value});
    if(V('content-output-text')) V('content-output-text').value=r.content;
    V('content-ai-output').style.display='block';
    toast('Generated! ✅','success');
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function saveContent() { const t=V('content-output-text')?.value; if(!t){toast('Nothing to save','error');return;} try{await api('/messages','POST',{body:t,channel:'email',ai_generated:1});toast('Saved to CRM!','success');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');} }

// VOICE AI
let isRecording=false, mediaRecorder, audioChunks=[];
views['voice-ai'] = function() {
  content(`<div class="page-header"><div><div class="page-title">🎙️ Voice AI</div><div class="page-subtitle">Record, transcribe, and analyze meetings with AI</div></div></div>
    <div class="grid grid-2">
      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px">🎤 Meeting Recorder</div>
        <div style="text-align:center;padding:24px 0">
          <div id="rec-icon" style="font-size:56px;margin-bottom:12px">🎤</div>
          <div id="rec-status" style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:16px">Click to start recording</div>
          <button id="rec-btn" class="btn btn-lg" style="background:var(--red);color:#fff;border-radius:50%;width:70px;height:70px;padding:0;justify-content:center;font-size:24px" onclick="toggleRecording()">⏺</button>
        </div>
        <div id="rec-result"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="card">
        <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:12px">📝 Transcript Analysis</div>
        <div class="form-group"><label>Paste transcript / meeting notes — or record a voice note 🎙️</label><textarea id="voice-transcript" rows="7" placeholder="Paste transcript here, or click Record and speak..."></textarea></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px;align-items:center">
          <button class="btn btn-gmail btn-sm" id="voice-record-btn" onclick="toggleVoiceRecord()">🎙️ Record Voice Note</button>
          <span id="voice-record-status" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">Works in Chrome/Edge (free, on-device speech recognition)</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="aiSummarizeTranscript()">🤖 Summarize</button>
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="createTasksFromTranscript()">✅ Create Tasks</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="aiActionItems()">✅ Action Items</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="aiFollowUp()">📧 Draft Follow-up</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="aiInsights()">💡 Key Insights</button>
        </div>
        <div id="voice-output" style="margin-top:12px"></div>
      </div>
    </div>`);
};
function toggleRecording() {
  if(!isRecording) {
    navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia({audio:true}).then(stream=>{
      mediaRecorder=new MediaRecorder(stream); audioChunks=[];
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable=e=>audioChunks.push(e.data);
      mediaRecorder.onstop=()=>{
        stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());
        const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(audioChunks,{type:'audio/wav'}));
        V('rec-result').innerHTML=`<audio controls src="${url}" style="width:100%;margin-top:12px"></audio><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:8px">ℹ️ Paste the transcript below to use AI analysis</div>`;
      };
      mediaRecorder.start(); isRecording=true;
      V('rec-btn').textContent='⏹'; V('rec-btn').style.background='var(--green)';
      V('rec-status').textContent='Recording... click to stop'; V('rec-icon').textContent='🔴';
    }).catch(e=>toast('Microphone denied: '+e.message,'error'));
  } else {
    mediaRecorder?.stop(); isRecording=false;
    V('rec-btn').textContent='⏺'; V('rec-btn').style.background='var(--red)';
    V('rec-status').textContent='Stopped.'; V('rec-icon').textContent='🎤';
  }
}
// ── VOICE NOTES → TRANSCRIPT → AI (free Web Speech API, Chrome/Edge) ──
let voiceRec = null;
function toggleVoiceRecord() {
  const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!SR) { toast('Voice recording needs Chrome or Edge','error'); return; }
  if (voiceRec && voiceRec.recording) { voiceRec.stop(); return; }
  voiceRec = new SR();
  voiceRec.lang = 'en-US';
  voiceRec.interimResults = false;
  voiceRec.maxAlternatives = 1;
  voiceRec.recording = true;
  const status = V('voice-record-status');
  const btn = V('voice-record-btn');
  if (status) status.textContent = '🔴 Listening… click again to stop';
  if (btn) btn.textContent = '⏹ Stop Recording';
  voiceRec.onresult = (e) => {
    const txt = e.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript || '';
    const ta = V('voice-transcript');
    if (ta && txt) { ta.value = (ta.value ? ta.value + '\n' : '') + txt; toast('Voice note captured ✅','success'); }
  };
  voiceRec.onerror = (e) => { if (status) status.textContent = '⚠️ ' + (e.error === 'not-allowed' ? 'Microphone blocked — allow access and retry' : e.error); };
  voiceRec.onend = () => { voiceRec.recording = false; if (status) status.textContent = 'Works in Chrome/Edge (free, on-device speech recognition)'; if (btn) btn.textContent = '🎙️ Record Voice Note'; };
  try { voiceRec.start(); } catch { /* already started */ }
}
async function aiSummarizeTranscript() { const t=V('voice-transcript')?.value; if(!t){toast('Paste transcript first','error');return;} try{const r=await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Summarize: key decisions, next steps, open questions. Transcript: "${t.slice(0,3000)}"`}); V('voice-output').innerHTML=`<div class="ai-insight"><div class="ai-insight-title">Summary</div><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;margin-top:8px;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`;}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');} }
async function createTasksFromTranscript() {
  const t = V('voice-transcript')?.value;
  if (!t) { toast('Paste or record a transcript first','error'); return; }
  toast('Creating tasks from your notes...','info',10000);
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/agent','POST',{ message: `Turn these notes into tasks (one per action item, with due dates when mentioned): ${t.slice(0,4000)}` });
    toast('✅ ' + (r.reply || 'Tasks created!'),'success',7000);
  } catch(e) { toast('Failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}
async function aiActionItems() { const t=V('voice-transcript')?.value; if(!t){toast('Paste transcript first','error');return;} try{const r=await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Extract all action items with owner and deadline from: "${t.slice(0,3000)}"`}); V('voice-output').innerHTML=`<div class="ai-insight"><div class="ai-insight-title">✅ Action Items</div><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;margin-top:8px;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`;}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');} }
async function aiFollowUp() { const t=V('voice-transcript')?.value; if(!t){toast('Paste transcript first','error');return;} try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'email',context:`Follow-up email based on meeting: ${t.slice(0,2000)}`}); V('voice-output').innerHTML=`<div class="ai-insight"><div class="ai-insight-title">📧 Follow-up Email</div><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;margin-top:8px;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`;}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');} }
async function aiInsights() { const t=V('voice-transcript')?.value; if(!t){toast('Paste transcript first','error');return;} try{const r=await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Extract key business insights, pain points, and opportunities from: "${t.slice(0,3000)}"`}); V('voice-output').innerHTML=`<div class="ai-insight"><div class="ai-insight-title">💡 Insights</div><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;margin-top:8px;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`;}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');} }

What can I help with?`);
  } else {
    const el=V('hub-messages'); if(el){el.innerHTML='';HUB_STATE.messages.forEach(m=>{if(m.role==='system')return;const d=document.createElement('div');d.className=`chat-msg ${m.role}`;const av=m.role==='user'?'<div class="msg-avatar user-av">👤</div>':'<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">🧠</div>';d.innerHTML=`${av}<div><div class="msg-bubble">${m.content.replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div></div>`;el.appendChild(d);});el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;}
  }
};
function addHubMsg(role,text,pushHistory=true) {
  if(pushHistory) HUB_STATE.messages.push({role,content:text});
  const el=V('hub-messages'); if(!el) return null;
  const t=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  const div=document.createElement('div'); div.className=`chat-msg ${role}`;
  const av=role==='user'?'<div class="msg-avatar user-av">👤</div>':'<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">🧠</div>';
  div.innerHTML=`${av}<div><div class="msg-bubble">${esc(text).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</div><div class="msg-time">${t}</div></div>`;
  el.appendChild(div); el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight; return div;
}
async function sendHubMsg() {
  const input=V('hub-input'); const text=input.value.trim(); if(!text) return;
  input.value=''; input.style.height='';
  addHubMsg('user',text);
  const el=V('hub-messages'); if(!el) return;
  const typing=document.createElement('div'); typing.className='chat-msg assistant';
  typing.innerHTML='<div class="msg-avatar ai-av">🧠</div><div class="chat-typing"><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div></div>';
  el.appendChild(typing); el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
  try {
    const ctx=`AI Hub context. User: ${STATE.user?.name}. Has Gmail: ${STATE.gmailConnected}.`;
    const res=await chatStreamFetch(HUB_STATE.messages.filter(m=>m.role!=='system').slice(-12), ctx);
    typing.remove();
    if(!res.ok){let m='AI request failed';try{m=(await res.json()).error||m;}catch{}addHubMsg('assistant','⚠️ '+m);return;}
    const aiDiv=addHubMsg('assistant','',false); const bubble=aiDiv?.querySelector('.msg-bubble');
    if(bubble) bubble.classList.add('streaming-text');
    const reader=res.body.getReader(); const decoder=new TextDecoder(); let full=''; let failed=false;
    while(true){const{done,value}=await reader.read();if(done)break;for(const line of decoder.decode(value).split('\n')){if(!line.startsWith('data: '))continue;try{const d=JSON.parse(line.slice(6));if(d.delta&&bubble){full+=d.delta;bubble.innerHTML=esc(full).replace(/\n/g,'<br>');el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;}if(d.done&&bubble){bubble.classList.remove('streaming-text');}if(d.error&&bubble){failed=true;bubble.textContent='⚠️ '+d.error;bubble.classList.remove('streaming-text');}}catch{}}}
    if(!failed){const last=HUB_STATE.messages[HUB_STATE.messages.length-1];if(last&&last.role==='assistant'&&last.content==='')HUB_STATE.messages[HUB_STATE.messages.length-1]={role:'assistant',content:full};else HUB_STATE.messages.push({role:'assistant',content:full});}
  } catch(e) { typing.remove(); addHubMsg('assistant','⚠️ AI unavailable. Check Settings → AI Providers.'); }
}
function clearHubChat(){HUB_STATE.messages=[];const el=V('hub-messages');if(el)el.innerHTML='';addHubMsg('assistant','Cleared. How can I help?');}
function hubQuickAsk(q){const i=V('hub-input');if(i){i.value=q;sendHubMsg();}}

catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}

async function aiEmailSequence(){openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📨 Email Sequence</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div class="form-group"><label>Goal</label><input id="seq-goal" placeholder="e.g. Nurture new leads into paying customers"></div><div class="form-group"><label>Emails</label><select id="seq-n"><option value="3">3 emails</option><option value="5" selected>5 emails</option><option value="7">7 emails</option></select></div></div><div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="doEmailSeq()">✨ Build</button></div>`);}

For each: Subject line, When to send (Day 1, Day 3...), Full email body. Format clearly with --- between emails.`,max_tokens:2000});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📨 Email Sequence</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2);max-height:600px;overflow-y:auto">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-xl');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}

  const tools={
    insights:aiHubInsights,leadscoring:aiScoreAllLeads,
    emailgen:()=>openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📧 Email Generator</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div class="form-row"><div class="form-group"><label>Type</label><select id="eg-type"><option value="email">Cold Outreach</option><option value="email">Follow-up</option><option value="proposal">Proposal</option></select></div><div class="form-group"><label>Tone</label><select id="eg-tone"><option>professional</option><option>friendly</option><option>urgent</option></select></div></div><div class="form-group"><label>Context *</label><textarea id="eg-ctx" rows="3" placeholder="What is this email about?"></textarea></div></div><div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="doEmailGen()">✨ Generate</button></div>`),

    salescoach:()=>openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🎯 Sales Coach</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div class="form-group"><label>Describe your situation</label><textarea id="sc-sit" rows="4" placeholder="e.g. Prospect was interested but went quiet after demo..."></textarea></div></div><div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-ai" onclick="doSalesCoach()">🎯 Get Coaching</button></div>`),

// New AI tool handlers
async function doLanding(){const t=V('lp-ctx')?.value;if(!t){toast('Describe your offer','error');return;}toast('Writing...','info');try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'landing_page',context:t});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🚀 Landing Page Copy</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2);max-height:600px;overflow-y:auto">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-xl');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doProduct(){const t=V('pd-ctx')?.value;if(!t){toast('Describe the product','error');return;}toast('Writing...','info');try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'product_description',context:t});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📦 Product Description</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-lg');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doHashtags(){const t=V('ht-topic')?.value;if(!t){toast('Enter a topic','error');return;}try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'hashtags',context:t});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">#️⃣ Hashtags</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:14px;line-height:2;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="margin-top:10px" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${escAttr(r.content)}');toast('Copied!','success')">📋 Copy All</button></div>`);}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doAgenda(){const t=V('ag-ctx')?.value;if(!t){toast('Enter the meeting purpose','error');return;}toast('Building...','info');try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'meeting_agenda',context:t});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🗓️ Agenda</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-lg');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doBlogOutline(){const t=V('bo-topic')?.value;if(!t){toast('Enter a topic','error');return;}toast('Outlining...','info');try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'blog_outline',context:t});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📑 Blog Outline</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-lg');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doPressRelease(){const t=V('pr-ctx')?.value;if(!t){toast('What are you announcing?','error');return;}toast('Writing...','info',8000);try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'press_release',context:t});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📰 Press Release</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2);max-height:600px;overflow-y:auto">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-xl');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doJobDesc(){const role=V('jd-role')?.value;const ctx=V('jd-ctx')?.value;if(!role){toast('Enter the role','error');return;}toast('Writing...','info',8000);try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:'job_description',context:`${role}. ${ctx||''}`});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">💼 ${esc(role)}</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`,'modal-lg');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doRewrite(){const t=V('rw-text')?.value;const m=V('rw-mode')?.value;if(!t){toast('Paste some text','error');return;}toast('Rewriting...','info');try{const r=await api('/ai/rewrite','POST',{text:t,mode:m});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">✨ Improved Text</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:10px"><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${escAttr(r.content)}');toast('Copied!','success')">📋 Copy</button><button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="saveSnippet('${escAttr(r.content)}')">📌 Save</button></div></div>`,'modal-lg');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}

// Image AI — free generation via Pollinations.ai (no key, no cost)
let lastGeneratedImage = '';
async function generateImage() {
  const prompt = V('img-prompt')?.value?.trim();
  if (!prompt) { toast('Describe the image first','error'); return; }
  const style = V('img-style')?.value || '';
  const size = V('img-size')?.value || '512x512';
  const [w, h] = size.split('x');
  const full = style ? `${prompt}, ${style} style` : prompt;
  const url = `https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/${encodeURIComponent(full)}?width=${w}&height=${h}&nologo=true&seed=${Math.floor(Math.random()*99999)}`;
  const box = V('img-preview');
  if (box) {
    box.style.display = 'block';
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="loading"><div class="spinner"></div>Generating (free AI — can take 10-30s)...</div>';
  }
  lastGeneratedImage = url;
  // warm up: load the image to verify it resolves, then show it
  const img = new Image();
  img.onload = () => {
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = `<img src="${url}" alt="${escAttr(prompt)}" style="width:100%;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--border)">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:10px">
        <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="generateImage()">🔄 Regenerate</button>
        <button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="downloadGeneratedImage()">⬇️ Download</button>
        <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" onclick="useImageInPost()">📱 Use in Post</button>
      </div>`;
    toast('Image generated! ✅','success');
  };
  img.onerror = () => {
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = `<div style="color:var(--red);font-size:13px">⚠️ The free image service is busy or unreachable — click Generate again in a few seconds, or try a different style.</div>`;
  };
  img.src = url;
}
function downloadGeneratedImage() {
  if (!lastGeneratedImage) return;
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = lastGeneratedImage; a.download = 'nexuscrm-image-' + Date.now() + '.png';
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
}
async function useImageInPost() {
  if (!lastGeneratedImage) { toast('Generate an image first','error'); return; }
  try {
    const r = await api('/social','POST',{platform:'social_instagram',content:'✨ New visual just created with AI! ✨\n\n(Image: ' + lastGeneratedImage + ')',ai_generated:1,status:'draft'});
    toast('Saved as a draft social post ✅','success'); closeModal();
  } catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}

// ── SNIPPETS LIBRARY (localStorage — your best AI outputs, reusable) ──
function getSnippets() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nx_snippets') || '[]'); } catch { return []; } }
function saveSnippet(text) {
  const snips = getSnippets();
  snips.unshift({ id: Date.now(), text: String(text || '').slice(0, 4000), at: new Date().toISOString() });
  localStorage.setItem('nx_snippets', JSON.stringify(snips.slice(0, 50)));
  toast('Saved to Snippets 📌','success');
}
function openSnippets() {
  const snips = getSnippets();
  openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📌 Snippets Library</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div class="form-group"><label>Save a new snippet</label><textarea id="snip-new" rows="3" placeholder="Paste any text you want to keep..."></textarea>
      <button class="btn btn-ai btn-sm" style="margin-top:8px" onclick="addSnippetNow()">📌 Save Snippet</button></div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text3);margin:14px 0 8px;font-weight:700">YOUR SNIPPETS (${snips.length})</div>
      <div style="max-height:340px;overflow-y:auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
        ${snips.length ? snips.map(x=>`<div class="card card-sm" style="padding:10px">
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);white-space:pre-wrap;max-height:90px;overflow-y:auto">${esc(x.text.slice(0,300))}${x.text.length>300?'…':''}</div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:8px">
            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${escAttr(x.text)}');toast('Copied!','success')">📋 Copy</button>
            <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteSnippet(${x.id})">✕</button>
          </div></div>`).join('') : '<div class="empty-state" style="padding:18px"><div class="empty-text">No snippets yet — use "Save to Snippets" on AI outputs.</div></div>'}
      </div>
    </div>`, 'modal-lg');
}
function addSnippetNow() {
  const t = V('snip-new')?.value?.trim();
  if (!t) { toast('Paste something first','error'); return; }
  saveSnippet(t);
  openSnippets();
}
function deleteSnippet(id) {
  const snips = getSnippets().filter(x => x.id !== id);
  localStorage.setItem('nx_snippets', JSON.stringify(snips));
  openSnippets();
}

    // Ask the agent to parse the transcript into actions

        <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:12px" onclick="closeModal();navigate('tasks')">View Tasks</button>
      </div>`, 'modal-lg');
  } catch(e) { toast('Meeting processing failed: '+e.message,'error'); }
}

function iaFileToDataUrl(file, cb) {
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = () => cb(reader.result);
  reader.onerror = () => toast('Could not read that image','error');
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}
async function doImageAnalysis(){
  const file = V('ia-file')?.files?.[0];
  let imageData = '';
  if (file) {
    toast('Reading image...','info',4000);
    imageData = await new Promise((res) => iaFileToDataUrl(file, res));
    if (V('ia-preview')) V('ia-preview').innerHTML = `<img src="${imageData}" style="max-width:100%;max-height:180px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--border)">`;
  }
  const url = V('ia-url')?.value?.trim() || '';
  const q = V('ia-q')?.value?.trim() || 'Describe this image. If there is text, transcribe it.';
  if (!url && !imageData) { toast('Add an image URL or upload an image','error'); return; }
  toast('AI is analyzing the image (vision models are slower)...','info',15000);
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/analyze-image','POST',{ url, image_data: imageData, question: q });
    closeModal();
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🖼️ Analysis</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px">
        <div style="flex-shrink:0">${imageData?`<img src="${imageData}" style="max-width:160px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--border)">`:url?`<img src="${escAttr(url)}" style="max-width:160px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--border)">`:''}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div>
      </div></div>`, 'modal-xl');
  } catch(e) {
    toast('Image analysis failed: '+e.message+' (tip: use a vision model like meta/llama-3.2-90b-vision-instruct)', 'error', 9000);
  }
}

async function doEmailGen(){const t=V('eg-type')?.value||'email';const tone=V('eg-tone')?.value;const ctx=V('eg-ctx')?.value;if(!ctx){toast('Add context','error');return;}try{const r=await api('/ai/generate','POST',{type:t,tone,context:ctx});closeModal();openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📧 Email</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2)">${esc(r.content)}</div></div>`);}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function doCalendar(){const t=V('cal-topic')?.value;if(!t){toast('Enter topic','error');return;}toast('Building calendar...','info',10000);try{const r=await api('/ai/complete','POST',{prompt:`Create a 7-day social media content calendar for: "${t}". Format each day exactly as: "DAY 1 - [Platform] - [Post text/hook]". Use LinkedIn, Twitter/X, Instagram and Facebook across the week.`,max_tokens:1600});closeModal();window.__calContent=r.content;openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">📅 7-Day Calendar</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--text2);max-height:420px;overflow-y:auto">${esc(r.content)}</div><button class="btn btn-success" style="margin-top:12px;width:100%" onclick="saveCalendarDrafts()">💾 Save all as Social Drafts</button></div>`,'modal-xl');}catch(e){toast(e.message,'error');}}
async function saveCalendarDrafts() {
  const content = window.__calContent || '';
  const dayBlocks = content.split(/DAY\s*\d+/i).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if (!dayBlocks.length) { toast('Nothing to save — generate the calendar first','error'); return; }
  let saved = 0;
  for (const block of dayBlocks) {
    const platform = (block.match(/linkedin/i) ? 'linkedin' : block.match(/twitter|x\b/i) ? 'twitter' : block.match(/instagram/i) ? 'instagram' : block.match(/facebook/i) ? 'facebook' : 'linkedin');
    const postText = block.replace(/^[-–—\s]*\[[^\]]*\]\s*/i, '').replace(/^[-–—\s]*/,'').slice(0, 2000);
    if (!postText) continue;
    try { await api('/social','POST',{platform, content: postText, status:'draft', ai_generated:1}); saved++; } catch {}
  }
  toast(`Saved ${saved} posts as social drafts ✅`,'success',6000);
  closeModal();
}

// SETTINGS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Catalog verified live against https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/models on 2026-08-27
// (the old meta/llama-3.1-*b-instruct, deepseek-r1/v3, phi-3-medium, gemma-2, qwen2 models
// were retired — llama-3.1-8b hit end-of-life on 2026-08-26 and now answers 410 Gone).
const NVIDIA_MODELS=['nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct','nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-30b-a3b','nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b','nvidia/nemotron-3-ultra-550b-a55b','nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-ultra-253b-v1','nvidia/nemotron-4-340b-instruct','deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4-flash-0731','deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4-pro-0813','moonshotai/kimi-k3','openai/gpt-oss-120b','openai/gpt-oss-20b','google/gemma-3-12b-it','mistralai/mistral-large-2-instruct','meta/llama-3.2-90b-vision-instruct','meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct'];
const NVIDIA_NAMES={'nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct':'Nemotron 70B — Best for Business','nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-30b-a3b':'Nemotron 3 Nano — Fastest (Free)','nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b':'Nemotron 3 Super — Great Balance','nvidia/nemotron-3-ultra-550b-a55b':'Nemotron 3 Ultra — Most Powerful','nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-ultra-253b-v1':'Nemotron Ultra 253B','nvidia/nemotron-4-340b-instruct':'Nemotron 340B — Max Accuracy','deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4-flash-0731':'DeepSeek V4 Flash — Fast','deepseek-ai/deepseek-v4-pro-0813':'DeepSeek V4 Pro — Reasoning','moonshotai/kimi-k3':'Kimi K3','openai/gpt-oss-120b':'GPT-OSS 120B','openai/gpt-oss-20b':'GPT-OSS 20B — Tiny & Fast','google/gemma-3-12b-it':'Gemma 3 12B','mistralai/mistral-large-2-instruct':'Mistral Large 2','meta/llama-3.2-90b-vision-instruct':'Llama 3.2 Vision 90B (images)','meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct':'Llama 3.2 Vision 11B (images)'};
const OPENAI_MODELS=['gpt-4o','gpt-4o-mini','gpt-4.1','gpt-4.1-mini','gpt-3.5-turbo','o3-mini'];
const OPENAI_NAMES={'gpt-4o':'GPT-4o (Best Quality)','gpt-4o-mini':'GPT-4o Mini (Fast, Affordable)','gpt-4.1':'GPT-4.1 (Latest)','gpt-4.1-mini':'GPT-4.1 Mini (Latest, Fast)','gpt-3.5-turbo':'GPT-3.5 Turbo (Cheapest)','o3-mini':'o3-mini (Reasoning)'};
// Human-friendly name for ANY model id (including live catalog entries the
// curated maps don't know): "meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct" → "Llama 3.1 8B Instruct (meta)".
function prettyModelName(id) {
  const known = NVIDIA_NAMES[id] || OPENAI_NAMES[id];
  if (known) return known;
  const parts = String(id || '').split('/');
  const org = parts.length > 1 ? parts[0] : '';
  const base = (parts[parts.length - 1] || id)
    .replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
  return org ? `${base} (${org})` : base;
}

  // your key — always current, never a hardcoded placeholder list).

  try { liveModels = await api('/ai/models').catch(() => null); } catch {}

  let openaiModels = (liveModels && Array.isArray(liveModels.openai) && liveModels.openai.length) ? liveModels.openai : OPENAI_MODELS;
  // Never drop the user's currently-selected model from the dropdown even if
  // it isn't in the live list (it still works — it was set deliberately).
  if (s.model && !nvidiaModels.includes(s.model)) nvidiaModels = [s.model, ...nvidiaModels];
  if (s.model && !openaiModels.includes(s.model)) openaiModels = [s.model, ...openaiModels];

          <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
            <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px">🌐 Backend URL</div>
            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:12px">Point this at your deployed Cloudflare Worker (see <code>backend/DEPLOY.md</code>) to switch from browser-only storage to a real, syncable database. Leave blank to keep using local-only mode.</div>
            <div class="form-group"><input id="backend-url-input" value="${localStorage.getItem('nx_backend_url')||''}" placeholder="https://nexuscrm-backend.your-subdomain.workers.dev/api"></div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
              <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="saveBackendURL()">Save & Reconnect</button>
              ${localStorage.getItem('nx_backend_url') ? `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="V('backend-url-input').value='';saveBackendURL()">Clear (go local-only)</button>` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="card">
            <div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:14px">ℹ️ System Info</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span style="color:var(--text2)">Version</span><span>NexusCRM V4.0</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span style="color:var(--text2)">Backend</span><span id="backend-status">Checking...</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span style="color:var(--text2)">Database</span><span>${BACKEND.available ? 'Cloudflare D1 (synced)' : 'localStorage (this browser only)'}</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span style="color:var(--text2)">WebSocket</span><span>${ws&&ws.readyState===1?'🟢 Connected':'⚪ Not used by this backend'}</span></div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between"><span style="color:var(--text2)">Gmail</span><span>${STATE.gmailConnected?'🟢 '+STATE.gmailEmail:'🔴 Not connected'}</span></div>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-top:14px">
              <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="checkBackend()">🔄 Check Backend</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>`);
  checkBackend();
  renderBackupBanner();
  loadProviderStatus();
  loadUsageMini();
};
async function loadUsageMini() {
  const el = V('ai-usage-mini'); if (!el) return;
  try {
    const u = await api('/ai/usage');
    const byOp = (u.by_op || []).slice(0, 6).map(x => `<span class="badge badge-purple" style="margin:2px">${esc(x.op)} · ${x.count}</span>`).join('') || '';
    el.innerHTML = `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px"><span>Today: <strong>${u.today||0}</strong> calls · <strong>${(u.tokens_today||0).toLocaleString()}</strong> tokens</span><span style="color:var(--text3)">All time: ${u.total||0}</span></div><div>${byOp || '<span style="color:var(--text3)">No AI calls yet</span>'}</div>`;
  } catch { el.innerHTML = ''; }
}
async function loadProviderStatus() {
  const el = V('provider-status'); if (!el) return;
  try {
    const p = await api('/ai/providers');
    const names = { nvidia: 'NVIDIA NIM', openai: 'OpenAI', custom: 'Custom' };
    const rows = Object.entries(p || {}).map(([k, v]) => {
      const state = v.status === 'ok' ? '<span class="badge badge-green">✅ healthy</span>'
        : v.status === 'cooldown' ? '<span class="badge badge-red">🧊 cooling down</span>'
        : v.status === 'degraded' ? '<span class="badge badge-yellow">⚠️ degraded</span>'
        : '<span class="badge badge-gray">untested</span>';
      return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">
        <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px">${names[k]||k} ${state}</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);text-align:right">${v.successes||0} ok / ${v.requests||0} calls${v.last_error?`<br><span style="color:var(--red)">${esc(String(v.last_error).slice(0,60))}</span>`:''}</div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    el.innerHTML = rows || '<div style="color:var(--text3);font-size:13px">No provider info yet.</div>';
  } catch { el.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--text3);font-size:13px">Provider stats appear once you make AI calls.</div>'; }
}

// Shown on the dashboard until the person either backs up or dismisses it
// for this browser session — nagging every single visit would just train
// people to ignore it, but it should never be silent-by-default either.
function offlineBanner() {
  if (!REAL_MODE() || BACKEND.available !== false) return '';
  return `<div style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.12);border:1px solid var(--red);color:var(--red);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;margin-bottom:16px;font-size:13px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
    <span>📡</span><span style="flex:1">Backend unreachable — you're viewing cached data. We retry automatically every 15s.</span>
    <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="pingBackend().then(()=>views.dashboard())">Retry now</button>
  </div>`;
}
function dashboardBackupNudge() {
  // With a real backend the data is in D1 — the local-storage nudge is moot.
  if (REAL_MODE()) return '';
  if (sessionStorage.getItem('nx_backup_nudge_dismissed')) return '';
  const last = lastBackupDate();
  const days = last ? Math.floor((Date.now() - last) / 86400000) : null;
  if (last && days < 14) return '';
  return `<div class="ai-insight" style="border-color:var(--yellow);margin-bottom:16px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px">
    <div><div class="ai-insight-title">⚠️ ${last ? `Your last backup was ${days} days ago` : "You haven't backed up your data yet"}</div>
    <div class="ai-insight-text">Everything here lives only in this browser — export a copy so you don't lose it.</div></div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-shrink:0">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="dismissBackupNudge()">Later</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="navigate('settings');setTimeout(()=>document.querySelector('[data-stab=data]')?.click(),50)">Back Up Now</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}
function dismissBackupNudge() { sessionStorage.setItem('nx_backup_nudge_dismissed','1'); views.dashboard(); }

// ── BACKUP / RESTORE ─────────────────────────────────────────
// The single most important reliability feature for a browser-only app:
// a way to get your data OUT before something clears local storage.
function lastBackupDate() {
  const t = localStorage.getItem('nx_last_backup');
  return t ? new Date(t) : null;
}
function renderBackupBanner() {
  const el = V('backup-status-banner'); if (!el) return;
  const last = lastBackupDate();
  const days = last ? Math.floor((Date.now() - last) / 86400000) : null;
  if (!last) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="ai-insight" style="border-color:var(--yellow);margin-bottom:4px"><div class="ai-insight-title">⚠️ No backup on file yet</div><div class="ai-insight-text">You've never exported your data. If this browser's storage is cleared, everything is gone for good — export a copy now.</div></div>`;
  } else if (days >= 7) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="ai-insight" style="border-color:var(--yellow);margin-bottom:4px"><div class="ai-insight-title">⚠️ Last backup was ${days} day${days===1?'':'s'} ago</div><div class="ai-insight-text">Worth exporting a fresh copy, especially before switching devices or browsers.</div></div>`;
  } else {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="ai-insight" style="margin-bottom:4px"><div class="ai-insight-title">✅ Backed up ${days===0?'today':days+' day'+(days===1?'':'s')+' ago'}</div><div class="ai-insight-text">Last export: ${last.toLocaleString()}</div></div>`;
  }
}
async function exportAllData() {
  try {
    let payload;
    if (BACKEND.available) {
      // Real backend: pull every resource fresh over the API rather than
      // reading local state, so the export actually reflects the synced
      // database — not just whatever this browser happened to cache.
      toast('Gathering your data...', 'info', 2000);
      const [contacts, deals, tasks, messages, appointments, reviews, workflows, invoices, social, subAccounts] = await Promise.all([
        api('/contacts?limit=2000'), api('/deals'), api('/tasks'), api('/messages'), api('/appointments'), api('/reviews'), api('/workflows'), api('/invoices'), api('/social'), api('/sub-accounts')
      ]);
      payload = { app:'NexusCRM', exported_at:new Date().toISOString(), version:2, source:'backend', data: {
        contacts: contacts.contacts, deals: deals.deals, tasks: tasks.tasks, messages: messages.messages,
        appointments: appointments.appointments, reviews: reviews.reviews, workflows: workflows.workflows,
        invoices: invoices.invoices, social: social.posts, subAccounts: subAccounts.accounts,
      }};
    } else {
      payload = { app:'NexusCRM', exported_at:new Date().toISOString(), version:1, source:'local', data: loadDB() };
    }
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload,null,2)], {type:'application/json'});
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = `nexuscrm-backup-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    localStorage.setItem('nx_last_backup', new Date().toISOString());
    toast("Backup downloaded — keep it somewhere safe. It contains your login info, so don't share it publicly. ✅",'success',6000);
    renderBackupBanner();
  } catch(e) { toast('Export failed: ' + e.message, 'error'); }
}
function importAllData(file) {
  if (!file) return;
  if (BACKEND.available) {
    toast("Restoring from a file isn't supported yet while connected to a real backend — this export is for safekeeping, not one-click restore. Re-create records manually, or restore the D1 database directly via wrangler if you have a schema-level backup.", 'error', 9000);
    if (V('import-file-input')) V('import-file-input').value = '';
    return;
  }
  if (!confirm('Restoring a backup REPLACES everything currently stored in this browser.\n\nHave you exported your CURRENT data first, if you want to keep it?\n\nClick OK to continue with the restore.')) {
    if (V('import-file-input')) V('import-file-input').value = '';
    return;
  }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (e) => {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
      const data = parsed && parsed.data ? parsed.data : parsed; // tolerate a raw DB dump too
      if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || !data.workspaces) throw new Error("This doesn't look like a local-mode NexusCRM backup file (it may be one exported from a real backend, which restores differently).");
      DB = data;
      localStorage.setItem(DB_KEY, JSON.stringify(DB));
      toast('Backup restored! Reloading...', 'success', 3000);
      setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 1200);
    } catch(err) { toast('Restore failed: ' + err.message, 'error', 6000); }
    finally { if (V('import-file-input')) V('import-file-input').value = ''; }
  };
  reader.onerror = () => toast('Could not read that file.', 'error');
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

function switchSettingsTab(id,el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="stab-"]').forEach(t=>t.style.display='none');
  V(`stab-${id}`).style.display='block';
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item[data-stab]').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('active'));
  el.classList.add('active');
}
function updateProviderUI() {
  const p=V('s-provider')?.value;
  V('openai-sec').style.display=p==='openai'?'block':'none';
  V('nvidia-sec').style.display=p==='nvidia'?'block':'none';
  V('custom-sec').style.display=p==='custom'?'block':'none';
}
// Ready-to-deploy Cloudflare Worker: adds the CORS headers NVIDIA/OpenAI's
// APIs don't send themselves, so the browser is allowed to read the
// response. Only forwards to an allow-listed set of hosts so it can't be
// abused as an open relay to arbitrary sites.
const PROXY_WORKER_CODE = "export default {\n"
  + "  async fetch(request) {\n"
  + "    const CORS = {\n"
  + "      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',\n"
  + "      'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',\n"
  + "      'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization'\n"
  + "    };\n"
  + "    if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });\n"
  + "\n"
  + "    const reqUrl = new URL(request.url);\n"
  + "    const target = reqUrl.searchParams.get('url');\n"
  + "    if (!target) return new Response('Missing ?url= parameter', { status: 400, headers: CORS });\n"
  + "\n"
  + "    let targetUrl;\n"
  + "    try { targetUrl = new URL(target); } catch (e) {\n"
  + "      return new Response('Invalid url parameter', { status: 400, headers: CORS });\n"
  + "    }\n"
  + "\n"
  + "    const ALLOWED_HOSTS = ['integrate.api.nvidia.com', 'api.openai.com'];\n"
  + "    if (!ALLOWED_HOSTS.includes(targetUrl.hostname)) {\n"
  + "      return new Response('Host not allowed: ' + targetUrl.hostname, { status: 403, headers: CORS });\n"
  + "    }\n"
  + "\n"
  + "    const upstream = await fetch(targetUrl.toString(), {\n"
  + "      method: request.method,\n"
  + "      headers: {\n"
  + "        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n"
  + "        'Authorization': request.headers.get('Authorization') || ''\n"
  + "      },\n"
  + "      body: request.method === 'POST' ? await request.text() : undefined\n"
  + "    });\n"
  + "\n"
  + "    const headers = new Headers(upstream.headers);\n"
  + "    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(CORS)) headers.set(k, v);\n"
  + "    return new Response(upstream.body, { status: upstream.status, headers });\n"
  + "  }\n"
  + "};\n";

function showProxyGuide() {
  openModal(`
    <div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🛠️ Fix "failed to fetch" (CORS proxy)</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div>
    <div class="modal-body">
      <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:12px">NVIDIA and OpenAI's APIs don't send the CORS headers a browser needs to call them directly — that's on their side (confirmed on NVIDIA's own developer forum), not a bug in this app or your key. A tiny free proxy fixes it by making the request on your behalf and adding the missing headers.</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px">1. Deploy this (free, ~2 minutes, no card required)</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);margin-bottom:8px">Go to <a href="https://workers.cloudflare.com" target="_blank" style="color:var(--accent)">workers.cloudflare.com</a> → sign up free → "Create Worker" → delete the sample code → paste the code below → Deploy.</div>
      <textarea readonly style="width:100%;height:180px;font-family:monospace;font-size:11px;background:var(--bg);color:var(--text2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:8px" onclick="this.select()">${PROXY_WORKER_CODE.replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</textarea>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin:8px 0 16px"><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="copyProxyCode()">📋 Copy code</button></div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px">2. Paste the Worker's URL here</div>
      <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text2)">Cloudflare gives you a URL like <code>https://nexus-proxy.yourname.workers.dev</code> — paste that into the "CORS Proxy URL" field above, Save Settings, then Test Connection.</div>
    </div>
  `);
}
function copyProxyCode() {
  navigator.clipboard?.writeText(PROXY_WORKER_CODE)
    .then(()=>toast('Copied — paste it into your Cloudflare Worker','success'))
    .catch(()=>toast('Copy failed — select the text in the box manually','error'));
}
// Force a fresh live model catalog fetch from the providers.
async function refreshModels() {
  toast('Fetching the latest models from NVIDIA/OpenAI...','info',6000);
  try {
    const r = await api('/ai/models?refresh=1');
    const n = (r.nvidia||[]).length, o = (r.openai||[]).length;
    // Honest reporting: say which catalogs are LIVE vs curated fallback.
    if (r.nvidia_live && r.openai_live) toast(`✅ Live catalogs refreshed — ${n} NVIDIA + ${o} OpenAI models`,'success',5000);
    else if (r.nvidia_live) toast(`✅ NVIDIA live catalog refreshed (${n} models). OpenAI shows the fallback list — set an OpenAI key for its live catalog.`,'success',7000);
    else if (r.openai_live) toast(`✅ OpenAI live catalog refreshed (${o} models). NVIDIA shows the fallback list — set an NVIDIA key for its live catalog.`,'success',7000);
    else toast('⚠️ Could not reach the live catalogs (no key set, or local mode without backend/proxy) — showing the curated fallback lists. Every model in them is verified available.', 'warning', 9000);
    views.settings();
  } catch(e) { toast('Could not refresh models: '+e.message,'error'); }
}
// Marks a key field as "cleared" (input emptied) so Save removes the key.
function clearAIKey(e, inputId) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const el = V(inputId);
  if (el) { el.value = ''; el.dataset.cleared = '1'; el.focus(); }
}
function keyUpdate(updates, field, value, wasSet) {
  // value === mask and not cleared → unchanged (keep)
  // value === '' and cleared → send '' (erase server-side)
  // anything else → new key
  if (value && value !== '••••••••') updates[field] = value;
  else if (value === '' && wasSet) updates[field] = '';
}
async function saveAISettings() {
  const provider=V('s-provider')?.value;
  const oSet = STATE.aiSettings?.openai_key_set;
  const nSet = STATE.aiSettings?.nvidia_key_set;
  const cSet = STATE.aiSettings?.custom_key_set;
  const updates={provider,model:provider==='openai'?V('s-model-openai')?.value:provider==='nvidia'?V('s-model-nvidia')?.value:(V('s-model-custom')?.value||''),temperature:parseFloat(V('s-temp')?.value)||0.7,max_tokens:parseInt(V('s-tokens')?.value)||2048,system_prompt:V('s-system-prompt')?.value,proxy_url:(V('s-proxy-url')?.value||'').trim(),
    auto_score_new_contacts:!!V('s-auto-score')?.checked, daily_digest_enabled:!!V('s-digest-enabled')?.checked,
    daily_digest_hour_utc:parseInt(V('s-digest-hour')?.value)??13, daily_call_cap:(()=>{const c=parseInt(V('s-call-cap')?.value);return Number.isFinite(c)&&c>=0?c:0;})(), brand_voice:V('s-brand-voice')?.value||''};
  keyUpdate(updates, 'openai_key', V('s-openai-key')?.value || '', oSet);
  keyUpdate(updates, 'nvidia_key', V('s-nvidia-key')?.value || '', nSet);
  if (provider === 'custom') {
    updates.custom_base_url=V('s-custom-url')?.value;
    keyUpdate(updates, 'custom_key', V('s-custom-key')?.value || '', cSet);
  }
  try {
    await api('/ai/settings','PATCH',updates);
    STATE.aiVerified = null;
    updateAIStatus('unverified');
    toast('Settings saved — testing connection...','info',3000);
    await loadAISettings();
    await testAIConnection(true);
  }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function testAIConnection(silent) {
  if (!silent) toast('Testing AI connection — actually calling the provider...','info',4000);
  try {
    const r=await api('/ai/health');
    const provider = STATE.aiSettings?.provider || 'openai';
    const current = r[provider];
    if (current) {
      if (current.status === 'ok') { STATE.aiVerified = { provider, status:'ok', at: Date.now() }; updateAIStatus('ok'); }
      else if (current.status === 'no_key') { STATE.aiVerified = null; updateAIStatus('none'); }
      else { STATE.aiVerified = { provider, status:'error', at: Date.now() }; updateAIStatus('error'); }
    }
    // Silent mode (auto-run after saving): update the dot + a small toast —
    // never pop a modal after every Save.
    if (silent) {
      const st = r[provider] || {};
      if (st.status === 'ok') toast(`AI connection OK ✅ (${st.model || provider})`,'success',4000);
      else if (st.status === 'error' && st.kind === 'cors_local') toast("⚠️ Local mode can't reach NVIDIA/OpenAI from the browser (CORS). Deploy the free backend or add the CORS proxy — see below.",'warning',8000);
      else if (st.status === 'error') toast('AI connection issue: ' + (st.message || '').slice(0, 90),'warning',7000);
      else toast('AI provider configured — run Test Connection for details','info',4000);
      return;
    }
    const localOnly = !REAL_MODE() && !BACKEND.available;
    const anyCorsLocal = (r.nvidia && r.nvidia.kind === 'cors_local') || (r.openai && r.openai.kind === 'cors_local');
    const lines=Object.entries(r).map(([k,v])=>{
      let line = `${k}: ${v.status==='ok'?'✅ Connected'+(v.model?` (${v.model})`:''):v.status==='no_key'?'🔑 No key configured':'❌ '+(v.message||'Failed')}`;
      if (v.status==='error' && v.kind === 'cors_local') line += '  → expected in local mode (browser CORS). See the explainer below 👇';
      if (v.status==='error' && /model|not found|404|410|end of life|retired/i.test(v.message||'')) line += '  → try switching the model to nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct (free tier) or gpt-4o-mini.';
      if (v.status==='error' && /unauthorized|invalid|401|403/i.test(v.message||'')) line += '  → double-check the key (NVIDIA keys start with nvapi-).';
      if (v.status==='error' && /rate|429|quota|credit/i.test(v.message||'')) line += '  → rate-limited or out of free credits; NVIDIA gives 1,000 free credits at build.nvidia.com — wait a bit and retry.';
      return line;
    });
    const corsExplainer = (localOnly && anyCorsLocal) ? `
      <div class="ai-insight" style="border-color:var(--yellow);margin:14px 0 4px">
        <div class="ai-insight-title">⚠️ "Couldn't reach the provider" in local mode = normal, not a broken key</div>
        <div class="ai-insight-text" style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.7">Browsers are <b>blocked by CORS</b> from calling NVIDIA/OpenAI directly — this is enforced by the browser itself and happens to every browser-only app, no matter how valid your key is. Two real fixes, both free:<br>
        <b>1) Deploy the free backend (recommended, ~15 min)</b> — AI calls then go server-to-server and CORS disappears entirely. <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="margin-top:6px" onclick="closeModal();navigate('settings');setTimeout(()=>document.querySelector('[data-stab=system]')?.click(),50)">⚙️ Go to Backend Setup</button><br>
        <b>2) Add the CORS proxy</b> — paste your proxy URL in the Connection box below, Save, then Test again. <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="margin-top:6px" onclick="closeModal();showProxyGuide()">🛠️ Show me the proxy</button></div>
      </div>` : '';
    openModal(`<div class="modal-header"><div class="modal-title">🧪 Connection Test</div><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:10px">Each provider was sent a real 1-token test request — this isn't just checking whether a key is saved.</div>${lines.map(l=>`<div style="padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-size:13px">${esc(l)}</div>`).join('')}${corsExplainer}</div>`);
  } catch(e) { toast('Test failed: '+e.message,'error'); updateAIStatus('error'); }
}
async function saveSMTP() {
  const data={resend_key:V('smtp-key')?.value||undefined, from_email:V('smtp-from-email')?.value, from_name:V('smtp-from')?.value};
  try { await api('/email/smtp','PATCH',data); toast('Email settings saved! ✅','success'); views.settings(); }
  catch(e) { toast(e.message,'error'); }
}
async function testSMTP() {
  const to=STATE.user?.email;
  if(!to){toast('No user email found','error');return;}
  try { await api('/email/send','POST',{to,subject:'NexusCRM Test Email',body:'Real email sending is working! 🎉'}); toast('Test email sent! Check your inbox.','success'); }
  catch(e) { toast('Send failed: '+e.message,'error',6000); }
}
function disconnectGmail() { STATE.gmailToken=null; STATE.gmailConnected=false; STATE.gmailEmail=null; localStorage.removeItem('nx_gmail_token'); V('gmail-topbar-btn').style.display='none'; toast('Gmail disconnected','info'); views.settings(); }
function resetGmailClientId() { localStorage.removeItem('nx_gmail_client_id'); toast('Client ID cleared','info'); views.settings(); }
// ─ checkBackend (completes settings view)
async function checkBackend() {
  const el = V('backend-status');
  if (!el) return;
  if (!localStorage.getItem('nx_backend_url') && window.location.hostname !== 'localhost' && window.location.hostname !== '127.0.0.1') {
    el.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--text2)">⚪ Local-only mode (no backend configured)</span>';
    BACKEND.available = false;
    return;
  }
  try {
    const r = await fetch(API + '/health');
    if (r.ok) {
      el.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:700">🟢 Online</span>';
      BACKEND.available = true;
    } else {
      el.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--red)">🔴 Error ' + r.status + '</span>';
      BACKEND.available = false;
    }
  } catch {
    el.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--yellow)">🟡 Configured but not reachable — check the URL and that it\'s deployed</span>';
    BACKEND.available = false;
  }
}
function saveBackendURL() {
  setBackendURL(V('backend-url-input')?.value || '');
  toast(localStorage.getItem('nx_backend_url') ? 'Backend URL saved — checking connection...' : 'Cleared — back to local-only mode.', 'info');
  views.settings();
}
